### 2026-09-07 17:03:49  #其他

【交通運輸部：將收費公路償債或者經營期間的收費標準調整為由省級人民政府審查批准】交通運輸部相關司局負責人就公路法修正草案徵求意見稿答記者問。徵求意見稿的主要內容包括三個方面：一是進一步規範相關表述。將“交通主管部門”統一修改為“交通運輸主管部門”，將“貧困地區”修改為“欠發達地區”，將“利用貸款或者集資”修改為“依法舉借債務”。二是進一步嚴格審批管理。將收費公路償債或者經營期間的收費標準，由省級人民政府交通主管部門會同同級物價行政主管部門審查批准，調整為由省級人民政府審查批准，強化政府責任，提高管理要求。三是進一步明確管理養護資金籌集渠道。明確收費公路在償債期間或者經營期間的管理養護費用，在車輛通行費中列支。明確高速公路和獨立橋樑、隧道償債期限或者經營期限屆滿後由政府統一管理，根據實際需要，可以採用收取車輛通行費等辦法籌集管理養護資金，收費標準由省級人民政府審查批准。對於納入市政管理養護的，以及財政能夠保障的則不再收費。（新華社）

### 2026-09-07 16:57:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

US Natgas Prices Hold Near 2-Month High — US natural gas prices remained above $2.90/MMBtu, not far from two-month highs, as traders balanced stronger cooling demand and rising LNG exports against plentiful domestic supplies. Persistent heat across the continental US continued to boost air-conditioning demand, supporting gas consumption in the power sector. Meanwhile, average feedgas flows to the nine major LNG export facilities climbed to 18.3 bcfd in early September from 17.2 bcfd in August, as facilities in Texas returned to full operations after maintenance. Export demand also strengthened in Europe and Asia, where buyers are replenishing inventories ahead of the winter heating season amid ongoing disruptions to LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf. However, high domestic production and comfortable storage levels limit further price gains. US gas inventories stood 5.2% above the five-year seasonal average as of August 28, while output across the Lower 48 remained close to record levels, keeping the market well supplied.

China’s FX Reserves Rise More than Expected — China’s foreign exchange reserves rose to $3.438 trillion in August 2026, up from $3.419 trillion a month earlier and above market expectations of $3.43 trillion. The increase came as the yuan strengthened 0.5% against the US dollar, while the greenback weakened 0.4% against a basket of major currencies. Meanwhile, the People’s Bank of China extended its gold-buying streak to a 22nd consecutive month, lifting its holdings to 76.73 million fine troy ounces from 76.08 million at the end of July. The value of China’s gold reserves rose to $350.08 billion at the end of August, from $306.35 billion a month earlier.

### 2026-09-07 16:48:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Bulgaria Q2 GDP Revised Slightly Higher — The Bulgarian economy grew 2.8% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, slightly above the preliminary estimate of 2.7% but slowing from a revised 3% in the previous quarter. It marked the weakest pace of expansion since Q1 2024, as domestic consumption growth slowed to 6.2% from 7.6% in Q1. Household consumption eased to 7.6% from 8.6%, while government spending fell 2.7% after rising 2.2% in Q1. Gross fixed capital formation also moderated to 8.4% from 8.7%. Net external demand made a positive contribution to GDP growth, as exports of goods and services rebounded to 0.2% after falling 7.1% in Q1, while imports edged up to 8.5% from 8.2% previously. On a seasonally quarterly basis, the economy expanded 0.7% in Q2, slightly above the preliminary estimate of 0.6% and unchanged from the previous quarter.

Crude Oil Fluctuates as US and Iran Exchange Fire — Crude oil fluctuated above $91 a barrel on Monday after surging 9.7% last week, as escalating tensions between Washington and Tehran raised fears of prolonged disruptions to energy shipments through the Strait of Hormuz. The US said it had struck three Iranian oil tankers over the weekend, destroying one, in retaliation for ballistic missile attacks on US Navy warships. Iran responded by threatening to establish a new restricted zone outside the strait, reportedly extending from the US Navy blockade line into parts of the Persian Gulf. Tehran also claimed to have targeted three tankers, although the reports could not be independently confirmed. US Energy Secretary Chris Wright said Washington would maintain its naval presence and blockade, aimed at limiting Iranian oil exports while supporting the safe passage of commercial vessels. Wright previously said that millions of barrels of crude were still moving through the Strait of Hormuz despite the escalating conflict.

Moreover, stronger US jobs data lifted expectations for a September Fed rate hike, keeping higher yields and a stronger dollar as additional headwinds for nickel. Meanwhile, lower cobalt payables for Indonesian MHP are squeezing HPAL margins and could prompt higher-cost producers to reduce output, offering some medium-term supply support.

Switzerland Jobless Rate Unchanged at 3% — Switzerland’s non-seasonally adjusted unemployment rate stood at 3.0% in August 2026, unchanged from the prior month. The number of unemployed people rose by 2,268 from a month earlier to 141,544. Unemployment rose across most cantons, with the largest increases recorded in Waadt (+879), Genf (+365), Zürich (+347), and Aargau (+193). The rise in unemployment was concentrated in the services sector (+1,895), particularly in health and social services. The jobless rate among young people aged 15 to 24 rose to 3.4%, the highest since March 2021, from 2.8% in July, with the number of unemployed youth rising by 2,384 to 14,678. The unemployment rate for workers aged 50 to 64 was steady at 2.7%, with the number of unemployed people in this age group falling by just 62 to 38,683. The number of registered job seekers edged up by 48 to 227,248, while reported job vacancies fell by 1,423 to 43,733. Seasonally adjusted, the jobless rate was unchanged at 3.1% for the fourth month in a row.

### 2026-09-07 14:13:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Romania Q2 GDP Shrinks 0.4%, Confirmed — Romania’s economy shrank by 0.4% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, confirming preliminary estimates and following a 1.2% contraction in the first quarter. The latest reading marked the second consecutive quarter of annual economic contraction, although the pace of decline moderated significantly from the previous quarter. Household spending declined 3.1%, while government spending shed 0.8%, and net external demand contributed negatively, as exports rose 1.4% while imports increased 2.6%. By contrast, gross fixed capital formation surged 16.5%. On the production side, output fell mainly in mining and quarrying (-4.2%), wholesale and retail trade (-4.5%), information and communication (-2.8%), and public administration and defense (-2.0%), while the strongest gains were recorded in construction (15.3%) and culture and recreation activities (11.8%). On a seasonally adjusted quarterly basis, the country’s GDP remained unchanged after a 0.1% contraction in the preceding quarter.

### 2026-09-07 14:10:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Sweden Inflation Rises Less Than Expected — Consumer prices in Sweden rose by 0.3% year-on-year in August 2026, slightly up from 0.2% in July, which was the lowest reading since May 2025, but well below market expectations for a 0.6% increase, according to preliminary estimates. “Preliminary figures show a slightly higher inflation rate according to the CPI,” said Caroline Neander, price statistician at Statistics Sweden. On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell by 0.3%, matching the previous month. Meanwhile, the CPI with a fixed interest rate (CPIF), the Riksbank’s target measure, held at 0.7% year-on-year, unchanged from the prior month but slightly below the expected 0.8% gain. Stripping out volatile energy prices, a measure closely watched by the central bank, inflation eased marginally to 0.5% compared to the same month a year earlier.

### 2026-09-07 14:06:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #House Price Index YoY #Importance 2

UK House Prices Fall for First Time Since 2023 — The Lloyds House Price Index showed UK house prices fell 0.4% year-on-year in August 2026, marking the first annual decline since November 2023, after rising 0.1% in July. Compared with the previous month, prices fell 0.2%, defying expectations of a 0.2% rise and following a 0.1% decrease in July, bringing the average property price to £298,468. Lloyds Mortgages Director Andrew Asaam said the housing market has faced a more difficult backdrop in recent months, with global events affecting inflation and borrowing costs, while fewer homes are changing hands as buyers and sellers remain cautious. Lloyds expects the market to remain fairly subdued in the months ahead, although wage growth and resilient employment should support demand. Regionally, Northern Ireland recorded the strongest annual growth at 6.9%, followed by Scotland at 3.5%, the North East at 2.7%, and the North West at 2.0%. Meanwhile, the South East posted the largest decline at 1.6%, followed by Greater London at 1.5%.

New Zealand Stocks 0.2% Lower at Close — NZX 50 shed 31 points, or 0.2%, to close at 13,942 on Monday, after gaining in the previous session, tracking a decline on Wall Street on Friday amid expectations that the Fed would raise interest rates at its upcoming meeting following stronger-than-expected labor data. Traders were cautious ahead of China’s CPI and PPI data, as well as trade figures for August, due later this week. Rising oil prices also pressured sentiment amid an escalating conflict in the Middle East, raising concerns over inflation and boosting bets that central banks would raise interest rates. Materials, healthcare, real estate, and communication services mainly weighed on the index, with decliners including Vulcan Steel (-4.6%), Fisher & Paykel (-1.2%), Hallenstein Glasson (-2.0%), ANZ Group (-0.9%), Mainfreight (-0.7%), and Westpac Banking Corp. (-0.6%).

【Deutsche Bank: ECB likely to hike in September; another 25bp possible in December】Deutsche Bank expects the ECB to hike at its September meeting and to add a further 25bp in December, citing persistent energy-related upside risks to inflation. The bank says its prior view that the deposit facility rate would peak at 2.5% (current 2.25%) is under challenge and now judges a 2.75% terminal rate more likely. It adds that quicker easing of geopolitical tensions, including the Iran conflict, or weaker growth could keep the peak at 2.5%; absent broader inflationary pressures there is no basis to expect rates above 3%. The ECB decision is due Sept. 10, when markets widely expect a hike.

Japan Coincident Index Highest Since 2018 — Japan's coincident economic index, a key gauge of current economic conditions based on indicators such as industrial production, employment, and retail sales, rose to 120.6 in July 2025 from a final 118.9 in June, preliminary data showed. It was the highest reading since October 2018, pointing to a solid recovery in economic activity, supported in part by government measures to curb fuel and oil price spikes amid Middle East tensions, as well as a new fiscal policy framework aimed at balancing growth with budget sustainability. Meanwhile, the Bank of Japan kept its short-term policy rate at 1.0% in July, its highest level since September 1995, while judging risks to economic activity to be broadly balanced.

### 2026-09-07 13:30:05  #MKT News #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent #XAUUSD

【Iran restricted zone in Strait of Hormuz may be tied to Oman deal, could raise shipping risk】Stephen Zunes, founding director of Middle East studies at the University of San Francisco, says Iran’s plan to designate a restricted zone in the Strait of Hormuz may be linked to a transit-channel agreement under negotiation with Oman. If an Oman deal is feasible and acceptable to the US, Iran could scale back enforcement; if not, Tehran appears prepared to enforce the zone, making US rejection of the pact more difficult. A designated restricted area would expand the patrol and policing area for US forces and could deter shipowners and insurers from transiting the waterway, raising risk premia, insurance costs or rerouting. Gulf neighbours have a direct interest; if an Iran‑Oman agreement materially boosts shipping throughput they may support it — including via transit fees — and Oman and other Gulf states have been consulting on shaping Oman's position.

【Hybrid rice seed yield hits record 530.4 kg/mu in Hunan demonstration plot】A measurement team led by the Hunan Society of Agronomy with experts from the China National Rice Research Institute and Wuhan University recorded 530.4 kg per mu from a large-scale hybrid rice seed-production demonstration plot in Wuyang town, Suining County, Shaoyang, Hunan — the first reported seed-production yield to exceed 1,000 jin per mu. The plot used the super-rice hybrid Shuangliang You 138. China’s hybrid rice planting area remains above 200 mln mu; cumulative promoted area exceeded 10 bln mu by 2025. High seed costs and long-running seed-production yields of around 160 kg/mu have been identified as a major constraint on the sector’s sustainable development.

### 2026-09-07 13:17:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Singapore Retail Sales Growth at 14-Month Low — Retail sales in Singapore grew by 1.5% year-on-year in July 2026, marking the softest rise since May 2025 and slowing from a 4% increase in the previous month. The slowdown was mainly driven by declines in sales for department stores (-3.5% vs -9.8% in June), food and alcohol (-5.1% vs -1.2%), optical goods and books (-2.2% vs 0%), and other goods (-4% vs 1.1%). At the same time, turnover growth moderated for motor vehicles, parts and accessories (1.4% vs 3.6%), cosmetics toiletries and medical goods (4.5% vs 8.7%), and computer and telecommunications equipment (5.1% vs 11.6%). In contrast, revenues accelerated for recreational goods (13.9% vs 7.6%) and watches and jewelry (11.1% vs 10.6%). Meanwhile, total retail sales, excluding motor vehicles, parts and accessories, rose by 1.5%, following a 4.1% gain in June. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail activity increased by 0.9%, the same pace as in the preceding period.

### 2026-09-07 13:14:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Estonia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Estonia Inflation Rate Lowest Since 2021 — The annual inflation rate in Estonia eased to 1.5% in August 2026, the lowest reading since March 2021, and sharply down from 2.2% in July. It also marked the first month that inflation was below the ECB’s medium-term inflation target of 2% in over five years. The main downward pressure came from a further decline in the cost of food and non-alcoholic beverages (-2.6% vs -1.6% in July), driven by lower prices of fruits and nuts, milk, dairy products, and eggs. Prices also went down for clothing and footwear (-4.6% vs -3.1%), household goods (-0.8% vs 0.1%), and recreation and culture (-3.4% vs -2.6%). In addition, housing inflation moderated to 5.5% from 8.3%. Meanwhile, costs accelerated for transport (7.2% vs 5.4%), driven by an increase in the prices of gasoline (+7.9%) and diesel fuel (+27.9%). Inflation also went up for health (3.1% vs 2.7%) and restaurants and hotels (2% vs 1.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices grew 0.4%, following a 0.5% rise in the previous period.

### 2026-09-07 13:08:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

Japan Leading Economic Index Highest Since 2014 — Japan’s leading economic index, which gauges the outlook for economic activity in the coming months using indicators such as job offers and consumer sentiment, increased to 117.9 in July 2026 from a downwardly revised 116.2 in June, preliminary estimates showed. It was the highest level since January 2014, supported by the government’s record fiscal budget, which included comprehensive measures to ease cost-of-living pressures and emergency energy support to cushion the impact of the Middle East conflict, while inflation remained close to the BOJ’s 2% target. Meanwhile, the unemployment rate declined to 2.4% in July, its lowest level since July 2025, pointing to resilient labor market conditions, although employment was at a four-month low. Consumer confidence also improved to a six-month high, amid households’ stronger views on overall livelihood.

Since August prices have rebounded sharply to roughly 6.9 yuan/jin; the company estimates poultry turned profitable in August. Q3 is the seasonal meat‑chicken peak and Wens expects further cyclical upside from back‑to‑school institutional catering and holiday stocking, forecasting the poultry business should contribute a reasonable full‑year profit. Wens expects 2027 poultry conditions to be better than 2026. The company’s 2026 broiler sales target is a 5–10% increase versus 2025, with a medium‑to‑long‑term sales goal of 1.8 bln birds. 【Empty Qatari LNG carriers return to Persian Gulf, may signal restart of Strait of Hormuz exports】Vessel-tracking shows six Qatar-linked empty LNG carriers are in the Gulf of Oman or inbound to the Persian Gulf; Kpler reports a seventh recently transited the Strait of Hormuz and is now in the Persian Gulf en route to Qatar. Qatar supplied about one-fifth of global LNG and has all but halted exports since a tanker was attacked in late July. The ship movements contrast with a marked rebound in Qatari oil exports over the past month. Movements suggest Doha may be repositioning tonnage to resume LNG exports via the Strait of Hormuz despite renewed U.S.-Iran tensions and Tehran’s plan to designate an exclusion zone outside the strait. QatarEnergy did not respond to requests for comment.

Sensex Starts Week Slightly Lower — India’s BSE Sensex edged down about 0.2% to 76,350 at the open on Monday, reversing gains from the previous session, weighed by weakness in the technology sector. Indian shares also came under pressure as investors assessed the prospect of higher US interest rates following stronger-than-expected US labour-market data, while Brent crude near $97 a barrel raised concerns over India’s import bill and inflation. Investors were also watching changes to pre-open session rules, which could increase volatility around the market open. Among individual stocks, Tata Consultancy Services shares fell 0.8% and remained in focus after the IT major announced plans to invest 700 billion rupees in an AI data-centre campus in Telangana, underscoring continued investment in AI infrastructure despite near-term concerns over US demand. Other notable laggards included Jindal Worldwide (-5.8%), New India Assurance (-1.5%), Infosys (-2.5%), Steel Authority of India (-3.2%), and HDFC Bank (-0.3%).

Palm Oil Holds Gains Ahead of Monthly Data — Malaysian palm oil futures hovered above MYR 4,950 per tonne, extending recent gains as firmer edible oil prices on Dalian markets and higher crude oil prices lifted sentiment amid heightened concerns over a prolonged supply disruption from the Middle East. Rising El Niño risks added a bullish factor, with drier conditions threatening Southeast Asian production. Output in top grower Indonesia is projected to fall 2.9% yoy in 2027. Demand prospects also improved in India, where refiners imported record volumes of soyoil and the most palm oil in six months ahead of festivals. However, gains were capped by a weaker ringgit and a lack of direction from Chicago markets, which were closed for a public holiday. Meanwhile, ample supply remained a headwind, with Malaysian inventories at a five-month high in July. Weak exports added pressure, as cargo surveyors noted August shipments fell 6.5–14.9% from July. Traders now brace for monthly data from the Malaysian Palm Oil Board later this week.

### 2026-09-07 12:12:07  #宏觀

【工信部：下決心培育一批千億級規模的歷史經典產業叢集】9月7日，國務院新聞辦公室舉行介紹歷史經典產業高質量發展有關情況的新聞釋出會。工業和資訊化部消費品工業司負責人馮海滄回答了澎湃新聞關於如何將歷史經典產業文化底蘊轉化為市場競爭力的問題。

他介紹，工信部從兩方面下功夫。

一是在品牌培育上。從企業來說，我們要加快打造中國消費名牌方陣，支援更多歷史經典企業入選中國消費名品，推動建設一批世界一流的經典品牌。從行業來說，蘇州刺繡、龍泉青瓷等經營主體大多手藝精湛但規模不大，以中小企業為主，我們要鼓勵這些優質中小企業做精做深。同時要支援這類企業引入現代化管理方式與生產方式，走“專精特新”的發展路子。從產業來說，我們主要是最佳化產業區域佈局，下決心培育一批千億級規模的歷史經典產業叢集，提升區域產業集聚化發展水平。

二是在品牌宣傳上，主要是抓好“四個一”，組織好一次會議，就是首屆歷史經典產業博覽會；開展好一個活動，中國消費名品全國行；組織好一次大賽，中國消費名品產業創新大賽；推動成立一個組織，國際絲綢組織，鼓勵企業參加國家級展覽展示活動，拓展歷史經典產業的海外宣傳渠道，積極推動歷史經典產品融入國際高階消費市場。另外，我們正在積極推進“四個一百”傳播推廣行動，打造百名推薦官、拍攝百集微紀錄片、製作百集AI短劇、開發百條歷史經典產品體驗路線，透過這一系列舉措，深度挖掘國產品牌成長鮮活素材，講好植根於中華文化、立足創新實踐的新時代中國歷史經典產業品牌故事。（澎湃）

【多家航司推出付費飛機餐 機上餐飲開啟多元選擇】中秋國慶假期臨近，您發現了嗎？除了以往的免費飛機餐，付費定製飛機餐正成為新選擇。煲仔飯、牛排、煙燻三文魚、冒菜……陸續“飛”上雲端，打破了傳統飛機餐的固有印象。國航、東航、南航等多家航司密集加碼付費飛機餐賽道。從十幾元的平價輕食、四五十元的家常套餐，到數百元的高階魚子醬大餐，機上餐飲告別“千機一味”。有旅客表示：“推出個性化服務，給有需要的人更多選擇空間，我覺得也不錯。”也有常旅客說：“有時候吃飯沒點，能提供這種服務，對我們來說蠻好的。”面對付費飛機餐的興起，中國航協早就表示，免費飛機餐不會取消。而在消費分化的趨勢下，免費飛機餐的品質底線又能否守住？相關法律專家表示，航空公司在推出付費飛機餐的同時，如果透過變相手段，降低原有免費飛機餐的標準或者強制旅客購買付費餐食，則是損害消費者合法權益的行為，要在原有基礎上做“加法”，而不能做“減法”，讓旅客在充分知情的情況下去自主選擇。 （央視財經）

Copper Eases on Fed Rate Hike Concerns — Copper futures slipped to around $6.55 per pound on Monday, ending a two-day rally as stronger-than-expected US jobs data strengthened expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike this month, weighing on the demand outlook for industrial metals. Investors also assessed rising inflation risks after oil prices extended their gains as the US and Iran exchanged strikes on ships over the weekend. Still, copper remained near record highs amid persistent concerns over supply. Analysts pointed to a recent export ban in Congo, weaker production from major producers Chile and Peru, and disruptions associated with El Nino. Data showed top producer Chile recorded its weakest second-quarter output in at least 19 years. The country also lowered its full-year production forecast for a second consecutive quarter and now expects output to decline 2.6%. Elsewhere, uncertainty over tariffs continues to encourage copper shipments into the US, pushing Comex inventories to record levels.

【中國塑膠加工工業協會倡議：警惕盲目擴產風險，推動隔膜產業理性發展】9月7日，中國塑膠加工工業協會發布《關於警惕盲目擴產風險 推動隔膜產業理性發展的倡議》。經歷一輪市場變化之後，隔膜行業迎來階段性回暖，市場信心有所修復，整體看行業即將迎來較好發展機會。但在行業景氣度回升的背景下，新一輪投資擴張熱潮也正在快速湧動，潛在風險應引起全行業高度警惕。中國塑膠加工工業協會向全行業發出風險預警，呼籲產業鏈各方認清週期規律，堅持理性投資，共同守護產業發展根基，並提出如下幾點倡議：第一，摒棄唯規模導向，審慎進行投資決策。企業計劃擴產新建專案，要立足自身技術實力、客戶基礎、資金承受能力，客觀研判全球真實需求，充分評估產能釋放週期、良率爬坡、技術迭代、海外壁壘等多重風險，不被短期市場熱度裹挾。應優先做優存量產能，把資金更多投向現有產線智慧化改造、工藝提升、高階產品研發。應謹慎上馬普通同質化產能，避免盲目鋪攤子、拼規模，防止投資變成無效沉沒資產。第二，堅持創新驅動，堅定差異化高階化發展。應鼓勵高階優質產能建設，把資源向技術攻關傾斜，聚焦超薄隔膜、高安全功能塗覆隔膜、適配半固態/固態電池的新型隔膜等產品方向發力，補齊高階產品短板，以產品競爭力獲取市場份額，而不是依靠簡單擴產打價格戰，避免陷入低端內卷。第三，科學出海佈局，理性開拓海外市場。應充分評估海外地緣政策環境、碳合規、供應鏈配套等市場和經營性風險，充分評估自身企業的要素優勢和抗風險能力，不把海外建廠當作化解國內產能壓力的惟一捷徑，穩妥規劃海外業務，防範海外投資可能的重大損失。下一步工作中，協會將持續做好行業產能供需監測預警相關服務，持續跟蹤在建、擬建專案動態，釋出行業供需風險提示，搭建行業交流平臺，推動行業資訊共享，引導企業客觀判斷市場。也呼籲各地政府在招商引資工作中，尊重產業發展客觀規律，統籌區域產業佈局，引導專案向高階化、差異化方向發展，審慎推動同質化常規隔膜產能落地，防範低水平重複建設，避免產業資源浪費。隔膜是動力電池、儲能電池的關鍵核心材料，事關我國新能源產業整體競爭力。行業發展來之不易，經歷過寒冬的洗禮，更應當吸取過往週期教訓。希望全行業企業樹立長遠大局觀，理性規劃投資與產能，共同規避產能過剩風險，推動行業從規模擴張轉向質量效益提升，實現產業健康可持續發展。

【報道：美軍在霍爾木茲海峽執行四個月的秘密掃雷任務】近幾個月，美國與伊朗在霍爾木茲海峽展開針鋒相對的打擊行動，與此同時，美國還在這條狹窄、具備重大戰略意義的海峽水域開展了一項更為隱秘的行動。 據瞭解此次行動的人士透露，美軍大多選擇在夜間，派遣海豹突擊隊潛水員、機器人艇以及專用水下載具進入這片危險的海峽，執行了為期四個月的掃雷任務。 爆炸物處置這一充滿風險的作業在水下的黑暗環境中開展，目的是消除一個關鍵阻礙因素。自2月份美國與以色列襲擊伊朗之後，該因素一直令商船不敢運載重要貨物通行海峽。 戰爭爆發以來，德黑蘭方面聲稱已經在海峽的國際水域以及阿曼水域佈設水雷。國際海事組織在6月表示，重要航道上大約已經佈設了80枚水雷。航道監測機構的資料顯示，至少自6月初開始，三艘常駐海灣、承擔反水雷任務的美國艦艇就已經離開該區域，部署至印度洋與太平洋海域。

### 2026-09-07 11:51:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Thailand Inflation Rate at 3-Month High — Thailand’s annual inflation rate accelerated for the first time in four months to 2.53% in August 2026, from 1.95% in July, surpassing market expectations of 2.2%. It was the fastest increase since May, boosted by higher domestic fuel prices amid rising oil prices due to the ongoing conflict in the Middle East. However, the latest reading remained within the Bank of Thailand’s 1%-3% target range. Non-food inflation rose by 2.23%, while food and non-alcoholic beverages inflation increased by 2.99%. Meanwhile, the annual core inflation rate, which excludes volatile energy and fresh food prices, accelerated to 1.44% in August 2026 from 1.34% in July, marking the highest reading since May 2023 and exceeding forecasts of 1.33%. Thailand's Ministry of Commerce maintained its 2026 headline inflation forecast at 1.5%-2.5%, with a 2.0% midpoint. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.56%, rebounding from a 0.73% decline in July and marking the fastest increase in four months.

【比特幣區塊鏈Liquid被駭客攻擊損失3.2億美元 約4,000枚比特幣遭竊】一個獲多家加密貨幣交易所使用的比特幣區塊鏈遭到駭客攻擊，損失達3.2億美元。成為近期一連串竊案的最新一起，動搖外界對數位資產安全性的信心。開發一個旨在加快交易速度區塊鏈的Liquid Network在X貼文指出，其Liquid Federation錢包持有約4,200枚比特幣，其中約4,000枚遭竊。Liquid表示，涉事者“據稱是白帽駭客(white-hat hacker)”；這類駭客在發現漏洞後會轉走資金，原意是之後歸還，通常會收取一定費用。Liquid Network表示，“Liquid錢包將受到影響，對於造成的任何不便，我們深表歉意，”並稱已暫停所有新交易。該聯盟(Liquid Federation)成員正積極設法解決問題，以便恢復網路正常運作。

### 2026-09-07 11:44:46  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【Long-slumping Chinese hog market posts rebound】China's long-slumping hog market has shown a recent rebound. In the final week of August national live hog prices rose for a third consecutive week; last week wholesale pork averaged 16.22 yuan/kg, up 0.7% WoW and down 17.9% YoY. Monitoring by the Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Affairs across 200 wholesale markets shows supply remains ample to slightly excessive; last week's modest price rise reflected slaughterhouse throughput controls rather than a shortage of hogs, according to Xinfadi market statistics manager Liu Tong. Market participants are cautiously optimistic: some forecasters expect accelerated sow culling in Q3 and see a potential profitability turnaround in 2027. In futures, long-dated hog contracts trade materially above near-month contracts. Pork-price recovery is plausible but expectations should be restrained.

【美貨機事故或致航班大幅延誤取消】當地時間9月6日下午，美國亞馬遜公司一架從波多黎各聖胡安起飛的波音767-300型貨運飛機，在邁阿密國際機場著陸時衝出跑道，撞上地面車輛並起火。事故已造成5人死亡，5人受傷被送往醫院，其中3人重傷。美國交通部長肖恩·達菲在社交媒體平臺發文提示該地區的旅客做好航班大幅延誤及可能取消的準備。亞馬遜公司發言人在一份宣告中表示，該貨機由貨運航空公司“21 Air”為亞馬遜公司執飛，目前亞馬遜公司正與當地政府和官員密切合作，以查明事故原因。據瞭解，這架飛機機齡32年，1994年至2015年間曾作為客機供多家航空公司運營。飛機衝出跑道的時速約為207公里。衝出跑道後碾過一條供機場周邊倉庫工人及其他企業使用的道路。約60輛消防車輛和200名應急人員趕到現場。救援人員 仍在處理飛機漏油問題。邁阿密國際機場一度暫停運營。不過目前，機場4條跑道中已有2條重新開放。（CCTV國際時訊）

【Nomura: Lululemon Q2 weakness; prefers Anta for China sportswear】Nomura reports Lululemon (LULU.O) posted FY2026 Q2 constant-currency revenue down 5% YoY to $2.42bn, missing the $2.46bn consensus. Operating income fell 13% YoY to $454m; operating margin dropped 1.9ppt to 18.8%. Management cut FY2026 revenue guidance to a 5–7% decline YoY, implying ~$10.35–10.5bn vs. market expected $10.98bn. Management flagged a slowdown in mainland China, saying negative social-media commentary on its China promotions and absence from post-618 discounting hurt e-commerce performance; it expects intense competition in North America and China, will prioritize full-price growth in China and will participate normally in Double 11, and still views mainland China as an expansion opportunity. Within its China sportswear coverage, Nomura prefers Anta (02020.HK), citing structural upside from outdoor and emerging sports and a multi-brand strategy that should help navigate industry competition.

【摩爾執行緒跌出新低 網下打新2577萬股解禁，1股至少仍賺300元】網下打新解禁股賣出，一股至少仍賺301.2元。9月7日，摩爾執行緒-U（688795.SH）早盤20cm跌停，跌停板價格為415.48元/股，創出上市以來新低。股價跌停背後，該公司首發網下配售限售股於9月7日解禁。早盤，該股票以498元/股的價格開盤，之後迅速下跌，4分鐘左右便跌停，這是摩爾執行緒上市以來首次跌停。該公司股票發行價為114.28元/股，上市第5個交易日最高衝至941.08元/股，創出新高，之後股價震盪下行，近半年時間每股股價在500元～770元區間震盪。但是9月7日首次跌破500元/股。就在9月7日，摩爾執行緒有2577.4510萬股網下配售限售股解禁，佔該公司股本總數的5.48%，這些股份限售期為自摩爾執行緒首次公開發行股票並上市之日起9個月。摩爾執行緒於2025年12月5日在科創板上市，因2026年9月5日為非交易日，故解禁日順延至下一交易日，也就是9月7日。若網下打新的限售股以跌停價415.48元/股賣出，那麼相較發行價114.28元/股，意味著1股賺301.2元。也就是說網下打新解禁股若拋售，1股至少仍能賺301.2元。（第一財經）

South Korean Won Nears Two-Year High — The South Korean won traded at around 1,345 per dollar, reaching its strongest level since October 2024, as strong export earnings supported the currency. South Korea’s cumulative exports reached a record $709.4 billion so far this year, already surpassing the 2025 annual record, with semiconductor exports rising 169.6% in January-August amid strong demand for AI-related chips. Continued foreign investment in Korean equities also supported demand for the won, reflecting stronger foreign appetite for Korean assets. At the same time, corporate dollar deposits at the country’s five major banks climbed to a record of roughly $77 billion as exporters accumulated foreign-currency proceeds and delayed converting them into won. The won has strengthened by roughly 250 won from its July low, highlighting the currency’s sharp recovery. Meanwhile, markets raised the probability of a September Fed rate hike to around 60% from roughly 50%, keeping the Fed outlook a headwind for the won.

### 2026-09-07 11:16:40  #MKT News #Commodities #Precious Metals #Industrial Metals #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #XAUUSD #Impact mixed #DXY #Impact bullish #S&P500 #Impact bearish

【China Post Securities: Fed likely to hike in September; post‑hike pullback a clear entry for precious metals】China Post Securities in a non‑ferrous metals report said a strong dollar is keeping base metals under pressure. US August nonfarm payrolls beat expectations, raising the probability of a September Fed rate hike. July core PCE remains below the 2% target; if August CPI does not move materially toward 2%, a September hike cannot be ruled out. The report sees little chance of consecutive hikes, so a September increase could mark the peak of tightening and represent bad news exhausted. From an allocation perspective, central banks are materially adding to gold reserves, making gold’s floor more evident. The house views a September hike as likely; if a hike triggers a price pullback, that would be a clear buying opportunity for precious metals.

### 2026-09-07 11:11:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 1

Indonesia Forex Reserves Hit 5-Month High — Indonesia’s foreign exchange reserves rose to USD 146.5 billion in August from USD 145.3 billion in the previous month. It marked the highest level since March, primarily driven by higher tax and services receipts and government withdrawals of external loans, which more than offset external debt repayments and Bank Indonesia’s Rupiah stabilisation measures amid persistent uncertainty in global financial markets. The reserves remained robust, covering 5.4 months of imports, or 5.3 months of imports and government external debt servicing, well above the international adequacy standard of around 3 months of imports. Bank Indonesia said the reserve buffer was sufficient to bolster external sector resilience and safeguard macroeconomic and financial stability. Looking ahead, it expects resilience to remain supported by adequate reserves and continued foreign capital inflows, amid positive investor sentiment toward Indonesia’s economic outlook and attractive returns.

【2026年中國公平競爭政策宣傳週啟動】9月7日上午，2026年中國公平競爭政策宣傳週（以下簡稱2026年競爭周）啟動儀式暨2026年全國公平競爭大會開幕式在陝西省西安市舉行。市場監管總局黨組成員、副局長束為，陝西省人民政府副省長武文罡出席並致辭，總局反壟斷總監王鐵漢主持啟動儀式。束為強調，要堅持以習近平新時代中國特色社會主義思想為指導，全面貫徹黨的二十大和二十屆歷次全會精神，深刻認識“十五五”時期公平競爭治理面臨的新形勢、新要求，持續完善公平競爭制度體系，強化公平競爭監管執法，深化全球競爭治理合作，推動我國公平競爭治理效能不斷提升，堅決維護優質優價、良性競爭的市場秩序，為縱深推進全國統一大市場建設和經濟高質量發展提供更有力支撐。2026年競爭周繼續以“統一大市場 公平競未來”為主題。國務院反壟斷反不正當競爭委員會辦公室、市場監管總局將會同相關部門和單位組織舉辦重點活動95項，集中展示各地區、各有關部門在保護和促進公平競爭、加快構建全國統一大市場、推動高質量發展等方面取得的積極成效。本次全國公平競爭大會是2026年競爭周的開場活動，是我國公平競爭領域重要的多邊交流平臺，旨在厚植公平競爭文化，加強國際競爭治理合作。來自巴西、法國、喬治亞、匈牙利、緬甸、阿曼、巴基斯坦、韓國、俄羅斯、新加坡、南非、烏茲別克、越南等國家（地區）的競爭執法機構代表，中國—中亞機制、歐亞經濟委員會、世界智慧財產權組織等國際組織代表，總局有關司局、國內有關政府部門、司法機關、地方市場監管部門代表，有關商會和行業協會、專家學者和企業代表等約300人參加活動。（市場監管總局）

Japan 10Y Yield Steadies on Hawkish BOJ Bets — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield steadied around 2.91% on Monday, ending a two-day decline as expectations grew that the Bank of Japan could raise interest rates this month. Takuji Aida, an economic adviser to Prime Minister Sanae Takaichi, said the BOJ is likely to hike rates in September and deliver another increase by January next year. The comments point to a growing recognition within the Takaichi administration, which has previously favored a dovish stance, that additional BOJ tightening may be needed to curb excessive yen weakness. Japanese bond yields have also climbed this year amid concerns that government spending could rise under Takaichi’s expansionary fiscal policy. Meanwhile, an unusual meeting of the management team at Japan’s Government Pension Investment Fund has fueled speculation that the $2 trillion investor could raise its target allocation to domestic bonds.

【韓國“儲存雙雄”異動】9月7日，日韓股市集體高開，韓國“儲存雙雄”股價大漲，SK海力士盤中漲超6%，三星電子盤中漲逾4%。 訊息面上，日前，OpenAI正式釋出新一代旗艦模型GPT-6 Astra，該軟體可自主操作軟體、完成程式設計/金融建模/製作簡報等長週期多步驟任務。 中信證券（香港）研報認為，受持續的AI資本開支、不斷上升的AI採用率和貨幣化支援的影響，預計2027—2028年儲存需求增長將超過供應增長。儘管儲存ASP（平均銷售價格）上漲幅度可能放緩，但大幅下跌的可能性極低。儲存供應商正專注於在更長時間內維持當前盈利能力，因此不太願意激進上調ASP，儘管預計未來幾個季度將持續存在供需緊平衡。再加上長期協議（LTA）採用率不斷上升，這應會支撐高位利潤並進一步推動重估。 華泰證券研報認為，中長期看，監管處置“靴子落地”後，韓股走勢仍更多取決於半導體基本面情況。（上海證券報）

【算力需求帶動半導體擴產提速 低技術門檻賽道或存在同質化投資苗頭】2025年下半年至今，AI算力帶來的結構性增量成為國內外產業鏈龍頭上調資本開支的核心動力。美光、三星等海外龍頭相繼擴產，國內相關上市公司再融資預案數量與募資規模同比也大幅提升。綜合SEMI、券商研報等資訊可看到，本輪全球半導體擴產並非市場炒作概念，是全球算力需求上行帶動的真實產業行動，行業不存在全賽道瘋狂擴產的過熱局面，不過，部分技術門檻相對偏低的賽道存在同質化投資苗頭。未來，行業也面臨供需時間錯配帶來的半導體強週期波動、AI商業化落地不確定性帶來的估值迴歸壓力等風險。業內人士表示，市場對於“半導體泡沫”的分歧，核心聚焦三個問題：需求是否具備持續性、資本屬於產業長期佈局還是短期投機、供給是否會在短期內氾濫。（新華財經）

【OpenAI同日釋出兩份檔案：AI加速時代，安全正在掉隊】在外界等待OpenAI就Agent自主入侵德國程式設計師網站DseWiki事件披露更多細節之際，當地時間9月6日，OpenAI釋出兩份檔案：宣佈公司已達到去年設定的目標，擁有能夠在人類指導下完成熟練研究員數天工作的“自動化研究實習生”；另一份則由首席科學家雅庫布·帕喬基（Jakub Pachocki）撰寫，聚焦前沿AI發展的安全困境，強調目前沒有任何實驗室解決足夠程度的對齊和監控問題。兩份檔案釋放的是OpenAI對當前AI發展階段的一次完整描述：AI已經開始反過來加速AI研發，但安全、對齊和人類控制能力並沒有被證明能夠以同樣速度增長。OpenAI所稱的“自動化研究實習生”並不是一個完全自主的科學家，而是能夠在人類指導下完成定義明確、通常需要熟練研究員數天才能完成的研究任務，且正朝著2028年3月建立“自動化AI研究員”的目標推進。（第一財經）

【IFA變得“太中國化”了嗎？IFA CEO正面回應】當地時間9月4日至8日，2026年柏林國際消費電子展（IFA）在德國柏林舉行。新浪科技《歐陸志》在展會現場對話IFA執行長萊夫·林德納（Leif Lindner）。今年展會上，一個尤其明顯的變化是：中國企業的存在感越來越強。有一種說法是，中國企業佔所有參展企業比例近五成，在IFA中的權重越來越高。“IFA是不是變得太中國化了？”面對這一頗具爭議的問題，林德納對新浪科技《歐陸志》回應說：“我們不在乎創新來自哪裡，我們只是在反映市場現實。” 他表示，IFA的使命就是將全球市場上最好的創新帶到柏林。“六年前，很多創新來自韓國；20年前，很多創新來自日本。如果明年大部分創新來自馬來西亞，也完全沒有問題。”（新浪科技）

Indonesian Equities Trade Higher Despite Volcano Disruptions — Indonesian stocks rose 30 points, or 0.4%, to 6,662 in early trading on Monday, rebounding from the prior decline as healthcare, energy, and infrastructure sectors lifted sentiment. Locally, concerns lingered over natural disasters, including Mount Anak Krakatau’s eruptions over the weekend, which heavily disrupted air travel and affected 170,000 people. 【2028年塑造100箇中國消費名品】國務院新聞辦公室今天上午（9月7日）舉行新聞釋出會，工業和資訊化部、商務部、文化和旅遊部和市場監管總局有關負責人表示將多措並舉，推動歷史經典產業高質量發展。歷史經典產業是指絲綢、茶葉、瓷器、中藥、釀造、工藝美術、文房四寶等文化底蘊深厚的中華傳統特色產業，是中華文明的智慧結晶和民族瑰寶。工業和資訊化部近日會同多部門聯合印發的《關於推動歷史經典產業高質量發展的意見》提出：到2028年，培育50家百億規模龍頭企業，塑造100箇中國消費名品，打造若干千億級規模特色產業叢集。到2030年，培育形成一批世界一流企業和知名品牌，推動歷史經典產業邁向全球價值鏈中高階。工業和資訊化部黨組成員、副部長謝遠生表示，目前正在依託打造中國消費名品方陣等方式梳理全國曆史經典產業資源，編制產業分佈地圖，鼓勵地方加快培育特色優勢產區。同時，支援在重點商圈、城市文化客廳等場所開設經典名品展示展銷專區。（央視新聞）

Yen Holds Rally on Hawkish BOJ Bets — The Japanese yen traded around 156 per dollar on Monday after gaining more than 2% last week, supported by growing expectations that the Bank of Japan will raise interest rates this month. Takuji Aida, an economic adviser to Prime Minister Sanae Takaichi, said the central bank is likely to hike rates in September and deliver another increase by January next year. The comments point to a widening recognition within the Takaichi administration, which has previously taken a dovish stance, that additional BOJ hikes may be needed to stem excessive yen weakness. The currency also benefited from the unwinding of carry trades, expectations for capital repatriation and increasing political pressure from the US. Meanwhile, data showed Japan’s foreign exchange reserves dropped by a record $79.6 billion in August following Tokyo’s largest-ever yen-buying intervention, with authorities spending about $99 billion between July 30 and August 26 to contain persistent weakness in the currency.

Australian Dollar Holds at 4-Month Top — The Australian dollar held above $0.72, near a four-month high as growing expectations of another interest rate hike by the Reserve Bank continued to support the currency, while the US dollar remained broadly weaker. The greenback struggled to sustain the brief lift it received from a ramp-up in US rate hike bets following Friday’s blowout US jobs report. Caution prevailed ahead of Friday’s US CPI report, which could influence expectations for the Fed’s policy path. In Australia, strong GDP growth and inflation data have increased expectations that the Reserve Bank will raise rates again at its meeting later this month. Markets are now pricing in a 66% chance of a quarter-point hike to 4.60%, up sharply from just 10% a couple of weeks ago, while the odds of a further increase to 4.85% have risen to 50%. Meanwhile, ongoing tensions in the Middle East have heightened concerns over broader inflationary pressures, raising the prospect that central banks could keep tightening policy.

New Zealand Dollar Edges Lower — The New Zealand dollar edged down to $0.586 on Monday after falling 0.6% last week, as the Reserve Bank of New Zealand’s cautious approach to further monetary tightening weighed on the currency. The RBNZ raised its official cash rate for a second consecutive meeting last week, but signalled that future increases would be gradual amid increasing risks to the economic outlook. Governor Anna Breman said the bank wants time to assess how previous rate hikes feed through to the real economy, while Assistant Governor Karen Silk indicated that policymakers are more likely to wait until December before raising rates again, prompting investors to reassess future interest-rate moves. Markets are now pricing an 80% chance of a pause in October, while a December hike remains largely priced in.

### 2026-09-07 09:54:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Job Advertisements #Importance 2

Australia Job Ads Accelerate — Australia’s ANZ–Indeed Job Ads rose 2.5% month -over-month in August 2026, quickening from July’s upwardly revised 1.9% gain and marking a second straight expansion. It was also the fastest pace since February, underscoring resilient labour demand despite higher borrowing costs. Seasonal hiring ahead of Christmas lifted retail and food service roles, while tech-related ads eased modestly, with softer demand in software, data, and analytics. ANZ Senior Economist Jasmine Zheng cautioned that momentum may fade as elevated rates restrain demand, expecting job ads to trend lower and unemployment to rise only modestly given the series’ resilience. Year-on-year, ads climbed 7.8% and remained 21.4% above their decade average, highlighting strength even as cyclical headwinds build.

Dollar Holds Steady as Fed Outlook Mulled — The dollar index held above 99 on Monday after rebounding slightly in the previous session, supported by stronger-than-expected US jobs data that reinforced expectations of a Federal Reserve interest rate hike in September. Data released Friday showed US nonfarm payrolls rose by 162,000 in August, following a revised increase of 23,000 in July and comfortably exceeding forecasts for a gain of 56,000. Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remained unchanged at 4.1%, while annual wage growth slowed to 3.1%, though the moderation was less pronounced than markets had expected. Investors raised the probability of a September Fed rate hike to around 60%, from roughly 50% before the jobs report. Attention now turns to US inflation figures due this week for further clues on the central bank’s policy path. The dollar also benefited from safe-haven demand as the US and Iran exchanged strikes on vessels over the weekend, sending oil prices higher.

South Korean Shares Rally on AI Optimism — The benchmark KOSPI climbed nearly 3% to around 6,885 on Monday, rallying for a third straight session, as gains in semiconductor stocks lifted the index. The PHLX semiconductor index rose 3.38% on expectations that OpenAI’s new GPT-6 Astra model will boost demand for memory chips. Samsung Electronics and SK hynix advanced nearly 4% and more than 6%, respectively, while SK Square rose 5.4%, Hyundai Motor gained 1.9%, Doosan Enerbility climbed 5.1%, Shinhan Financial Group added 2.3%, and LG Electronics surged 9.4%. At the same time, South Korea’s exports reached a record $709.4 billion so far this year, already surpassing the 2025 annual record, with semiconductor exports jumping 169.6% in January-August, reinforcing expectations for solid corporate earnings and economic growth. Meanwhile, heightened US-Iran tensions remained a risk, while stronger-than-expected US jobs data increased expectations for a September Fed rate hike and pushed Treasury yields higher.

Heating Oil Hovers Near Record Highs — US heating oil prices hovered near $4.60 per gallon, remaining close to the record high of $4.73 touched on September 2 and about 98% higher than a year earlier amid concerns over increasingly tight supplies. EIA data showed that distillate fuel stocks rose by 796 thousand barrels in the week ended August 28 but remained 14% below the five-year average for this time of year. This comes as demand is expected to rise as agricultural states enter their harvest and planting seasons, while the approaching winter heating season could put further pressure on distillate supplies. Reflecting the market's tightness, average US diesel prices have reached a record high. The market has remained constrained by escalating tensions in the Middle East, ongoing disruptions to Russian refining capacity, and an extended Russian diesel export ban, further limiting global distillate supplies.

### 2026-09-07 09:09:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Jumps as US-Iran Conflict Escalates — European natural gas prices jumped more than 4% to above €74/MWh on Monday, their highest level in more than three and a half years, amid escalating US-Iran tensions that could further disrupt energy supplies from the Persian Gulf. Iran said it had targeted oil tankers and several US-linked vessels and plans to establish a restricted maritime zone beyond the Strait of Hormuz in the coming days. This followed US strikes on Iranian oil tankers, which Washington said were carried out in retaliation for Tehran’s missile attacks on US Navy warships. The continuing tit-for-tat escalation is heightening concerns that Qatari LNG shipments to Europe could be delayed even further. Europe is approaching the end of its summer storage injection period, with gas storage facilities currently around 62% full, below the five-year seasonal average. This increases the risk of tighter competition for supplies, leaving the region more exposed to price spikes during the colder months.

【光大期貨：加息預期又升溫，黃金還能拿嗎？】上週貴金屬面臨的環境是美國經濟韌性，油價高漲下的通脹隱憂以及超預期非農強化下的加息回升預期，CME資料顯示加息預期仍在升溫通道中。若9月FOMC果真加息，將標誌著自2024年以來寬鬆週期的政策基調發生根本逆轉。然後市場對9月加息預期仍然只在60%以上，這也意味著加息並未完全定價，或者說市場不敢“過度”定價，這可能源於加息並不能解決因地緣政治衝突導致的油價高企的問題，亦存在美債問題的掣肘。因此我們看到，上週環境對黃金雖然不利，但黃金反彈之勢並未完全結束。綜合來看，9月美聯儲議息會議存在較大不確定性，黃金或更加關注美國經濟和通脹資料的表現，價格表現可能亦會反覆。建議採取區間思維的靈活配置思路。（光大期貨）

【五糧液普五八代、水晶劍南春創月新高 夢之藍M6+、古井貢古20兩連跌】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，9月7日中國白酒業11大單品的終端零售均價六漲五跌，上漲品種略佔優勢，盤面漲跌分化顯著。上漲方面，精品茅臺上漲2元至2458元，結束兩連陰；五糧液普五八代上漲4元至820元，錄得三連陽，價格漲至本月最高點；五糧液1618上漲2元至830元，昨天失守後迅速收復這一整十關口；青花汾20上漲1元至390元，三連跌後穩住陣腳；國窖1573上漲1元至886元，前一日技術性回撥後價盤溫和修復；水晶劍南春上漲4元至411元，二連陽創下近一個月價格新高。下跌方面，飛天茅臺下跌3元至1790元，連續三日小幅回撥；洋河夢之藍M6+下跌4元至614元，自月高位回撥已兩連陰；古井貢古20下跌5元至533元，M型震盪後加速下探；習酒君品下跌3元至641元，兩連跌自30天高位持續回落；青花郎下跌4元至689元，連續兩日走弱，跌破690元價位。

今日11大單品的終端零售總價小幅回落，上漲品種數量雖略佔上風，但輸家跌幅更重。分板塊看，高階與次高階酒品分化明顯，部分前期走強品種持續回探。如果11大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10062元，較昨日小幅下跌5元，連續兩日回落創下4天以來新低。

【韓國選定得州223億美元天然氣電廠作為首個美國投資專案】韓國經濟日報援引不願具名的政府訊息人士報道，韓國已選定美國得克薩斯州一項約223億美元燃氣發電廠專案，作為其3500億美元對美投資計劃下的首個落地專案。韓美雙方敲定專案最終造價約223億美元；美方最初要求投資250億美元，韓國方面最初考量金額為198億美元。該專案選址於得克薩斯州恩西納爾，總裝機容量6.3吉瓦，用於滿足人工智慧資料中心的電力需求。專案先建設1.4吉瓦燃氣輪機發電機組，後續再新增4.9吉瓦聯合迴圈發電產能。韓國政府計劃於週一向國會彙報該專案，完成國會報備與審批流程後，於本月正式公佈這一首個對美投資專案。

Japanese Shares Jump on Tech Boost — The Nikkei 225 Index jumped 2% to above 66,300 while the broader Topix Index gained 0.8% to 4,136 on Monday, with Japanese shares advancing for a second straight session as technology and artificial intelligence-linked stocks outperformed. The gains came as US chipmakers and memory stocks rallied on Friday amid improving sentiment toward the sector, although the broader market remained pressured by stronger-than-expected US jobs data that boosted expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. Markets also priced in a rate increase from the Bank of Japan at its September meeting as the country continues to grapple with persistent inflation and a weak yen. Top tech gainers included Kioxia Holdings (7.2%), SoftBank Group (6.1%), Advantest (2.6%), Fujikura (5.9%) and Tokyo Electron (5.3%). Meanwhile, financial and consumer stocks underperformed, with losses from Mitsubishi UFJ (-0.9%) and Nintendo (-2.2%).

【經濟日報：17億塊晶片的喜與憂】7月份，我國規模以上工業企業積體電路產量達530億塊，相當於日均生產約17億塊晶片；今年前7個月，我國積體電路出口總額達2160億美元，同比增長99.5%，已超2025年全年2019億美元的出口總額。這組資料表明，中國積體電路產業正迎來新一輪高速增長。欣喜之餘，我們仍需清醒看到行業隱憂。憂在何處？產業深層矛盾尚待破解。技術壁壘與“卡脖子”風險猶存。儘管晶片產量與出口飆升，但高階晶片仍受制於國外技術封鎖，關鍵材料、裝置國產化率不足，產業鏈安全存在隱患。產業結構隱憂顯現。當前產能增長主要依賴儲存晶片與成熟製程，中低端晶片產能集中、存在重複建設問題，高階晶片產能不足、存在結構性緊缺。國際競爭壓力持續加劇。美國對華技術圍堵不斷升級，全球半導體格局重塑充滿不確定性，出口高增長能否持續面臨考驗。面對機遇與挑戰並存的格局，中國積體電路產業需從“量的積累”轉向“質的突破”。錨定高階，突破“卡脖子”瓶頸。要持續加大研發投入，把資源向關鍵核心技術傾斜，聚焦裝置、材料等薄弱環節集中攻關，用好新型舉國體制，推動單項技術突破向全鏈條能力躍升。

【Japan's FX reserves record largest monthly drop in August】Japan's FX reserves posted a record monthly decline in August after Tokyo launched a fresh, large-scale dollar-selling/yen-buying intervention to counter yen weakness, the Finance Ministry said. Reserves fell to $1.208 tln at end-August from $1.287 tln a month earlier, a record drop of $79.6 bln, or 6.18%. The fall was driven largely by a sharp reduction in foreign-currency securities holdings, much of which are US Treasuries bought during dollar-buying interventions about 20 years ago and which account for roughly 70% of Japan's FX reserves.

### 2026-09-07 08:20:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Tunisia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Tunisia Inflation Rate Hits 3-Month High of 5.4% — Tunisia's annual inflation rate accelerated for the first time in three months to 5.4% in August 2026, from July’s four-month low of 5.1%. It was the highest inflation rate since May, mainly driven by faster rises in food & non-alcoholic beverages (7.5% vs 6.6% in July) and transportation prices (2.1% vs 2%). Prices also rose at a faster pace for health (3.4% vs 2.7%) and restaurants & hotels (6.6% vs 6.5%), while clothing & footwear prices eased slightly (9.0% vs 9.1%). Meanwhile, core inflation, which excludes food and energy, inched up to 4.9% from 4.8% in July. On a monthly basis, the CPI climbed by 0.5%, accelerating from a 0.2% rise in the previous month and marking the fastest increase in four months.

【中信證券：當前美債期限利差結構的變化總體仍屬“正常化”而非失控】中信證券研報稱，本輪發達市場長債上行並非單一通脹衝擊，而是實際利率、期限溢價、財政擴與央行政策同步重估的結果。當前美債期限利差結構的變化總體仍屬“正常化”而非失控，若美聯儲9月如期加息，且美國通脹與長債供給壓力延續，10年期、30年期美債收益率或分別向5.0%和5.5%靠攏，短期將繼續壓制高估值資產，中長期拐點仍取決於美國通脹回落速度以及財政鞏固政策。2015年以來的兩輪美聯儲加息週期，香港市場資金流入通常自加息前四周起逐步放緩，在加息當週降至低點，隨後逐步修復。但若全球長端利率繼續上行，港股高股息相對海外無風險資產的收益率優勢有所收窄，配置上應進一步考察現金流穩定性、盈利確定性和分紅可持續性，並關注南向持倉佔比較高、外資邊際定價權相對較低的細分方向，建議優先關注銀行、煤炭、物業管理和燃氣。

Silver Holds Decline on Fed Rate Hike Bets — Silver traded near $66 an ounce on Monday after losing more than 1% in the previous session, pressured by growing expectations of an imminent Federal Reserve interest rate hike following stronger-than-expected US jobs data last week. Data released Friday showed US nonfarm payrolls rose by 162,000 in August, following an upwardly revised increase of 23,000 in July and well above market expectations for a gain of 56,000. Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remained unchanged at 4.1%, while annual wage growth slowed to 3.1%, although the decline was smaller than economists had expected. Markets increased bets on a September Fed rate hike to roughly 60%, up from around 50% before the data release. Silver also remained under pressure from higher oil prices as the US and Iran exchanged strikes on ships over the weekend, stoking concerns about renewed inflationary pressures.

【俄稱日本有病別傳染給他國】俄羅斯外交部發言人扎哈羅娃9月6日直斥日本首相高市早苗關於“拆除俄羅斯一座紀念碑”的要求太荒謬。 俄羅斯遠東哈巴羅夫斯克市9月4日為一座紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑舉行揭幕儀式。日本首相高市早苗隨後在社交媒體平臺用日語和俄語發帖，要求俄方將紀念碑拆除。高市早苗在帖文中稱，“菊花紋章作為象徵日本的紋章被廣泛認知，設立表現其被摧毀場景的紀念碑完全無法接受。這是關乎日本國民感情的問題，日本政府此前已就此向俄方強烈要求停止設定並拆除該紀念碑。今後將繼續強烈敦促其拆除。” 據俄新社9月6日報道，扎哈羅娃就此發問，日本政府“接下來是不是該要求風不要吹、太陽不要發光了？” 扎哈羅娃還強調， “我們的歷史基於事實，我們的記憶鐫刻於紀念碑上。日方無視本國曆史，但別把這種‘病’傳染給其他國家。” 這是俄羅斯目前最大的、紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑，由俄羅斯軍事歷史學會與哈巴羅夫斯克邊疆區政府合作建成。紀念碑中央是一尊15米高的、手持長槍的蘇聯士兵雕像，旁邊一座繪有菊花圖案的界碑被砸成兩半。俄羅斯軍事歷史學會表示，被搗毀的菊花圖案正是軍國主義日本的象徵，如此設計紀念碑是正確和符合史實的。（CCTV國際時訊）

【Ministry of Finance to support central financial firms' core Tier‑1 capital replenishment】China's Ministry of Finance said it will issue 300 billion yuan of special treasury bonds in the near term to support eight central financial firms in replenishing core Tier‑1 capital: Industrial and Commercial Bank of China, Agricultural Bank of China, China Exim Bank, Sinosure, PICC, China Life, China Taiping and China Re. The operation will be carried out on market‑based, rule‑of‑law principles, the ministry said, noting the eight firms currently report steady operations, stable asset quality and regulatory metrics within a safe range. The bond‑backed injections are intended to strengthen their solvency, risk‑absorption capacity and ability to serve the real economy, support high‑quality development and underpin stable economic growth; for listed central financial firms, the ministry added, the support should help generate greater investor value and deliver long‑term stable returns.

### 2026-09-07 07:57:45  #其他

【經濟日報金觀平：用好涉稅資訊規範平臺經濟】稅務部門最新資料顯示，《網際網路平臺企業涉稅資訊報送規定》實施一年多來，近9700家境內外平臺企業積極履行涉稅資訊報送義務，平臺經濟規範健康發展邁出堅實一步。長期以來，由於交易虛擬性、資料不透明、資金流轉複雜等特點，網際網路平臺滋生出刷單炒信、不實申報等違規行為。一些地方還利用平臺企業“註冊地與經營地分離”的資訊壁壘，以“開票經濟”虛增收入，妨礙全國統一大市場建設。新規實施一年多來，制度監管下的合規行為正逐步轉化為網際網路平臺及平臺內經營者的內在自覺。與去年10月首次報送時相比，今年一季度報送涉稅資訊的平臺數量增長39%，報送的平臺內經營者和從業人員資訊分別增長21%、19%。用好資料，重點在“精準”。要透過多部門涉稅資訊共享和協同共治，進一步實現對涉稅違法行為從事後打擊向事前事中精準防範轉變，合力提升稅收服務與管理效能，有力有效保護納稅人合法權益。利用海量資料完善風險模型，實時掃描異常並主動提醒，加強對苗頭性、傾向性問題的研判和防範。針對“開票經濟”亂象，強化部門聯動，打破資訊壁壘，透過交叉比對多維資料、建立動態風險指標模型、強化聯合懲戒等措施，精準查處“空轉走單”“迴圈開票”等違規行為，擠出數字水分，為高質量發展營造公平健康環境。

### 2026-09-07 07:56:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 2

Japan Forex Reserves Hit Nearly 4-Year Low — Japan’s foreign reserves sank by USD 79.58 billion to USD 1.208 trillion at the end of August 2026 from USD 1.287 trillion in July, the lowest level since October 2022, as large-scale yen-buying intervention reduced foreign-currency assets. The Ministry of Finance reported ¥15.4 trillion of foreign-exchange intervention between July 30 and August 26 as authorities moved to support the yen after it weakened sharply. Foreign currency reserves stood at USD 994.98 billion, consisting of USD 839.56 billion in securities and USD 155.42 billion in deposits. Of the deposits, USD 154.98 billion were held with foreign central banks and the Bank for International Settlements (BIS), while deposits with banks headquartered in Japan totaled USD 409 million and those with banks headquartered outside Japan amounted to USD 25 million. Japan's IMF reserve position stood at USD 11.42 billion, SDR holdings were valued at USD 61.17 billion, and gold reserves totaled USD 124.10 billion.

【“六張網”建設密集推進 下半年實物工作量集中釋放】近期，“六張網”建設迎來政策密集落地訊號。8月27日，《物流網建設實施方案》正式印發，成為六網體系中首個對外發布的國家級專項實施方案，標誌著“六張網”從頂層設計全面轉入分網施工、分批落地的攻堅階段。8月28日召開的全國投資工作推進會議和9月2日召開的“六張網”重大專案協調推進機制會，接連釋放加快新型政策性金融工具投放、抓緊形成實物工作量的政策訊號。國家發展改革委國家資訊中心經濟預測部政策模擬實驗室主任、研究員肖宏偉向證券時報記者表示，從落地節奏看，今年三季度與四季度將呈現差異化安排。三季度是施工黃金期，地方層面將加快在建專案進度、推動擬建專案儘早落地；四季度則將迎來重大專案集中開工的高峰期，對總需求的帶動作用更加明顯。隨著資金撥付逐步到位、前期儲備專案成熟度提升，更多實物工作量將在下半年集中釋放。全年超7萬億元級的投資盤子裡，超六成工作量有望集中在下半年兌現。

Brent Rises as US and Iran Exchange Fire — Brent crude rose toward $97 per barrel on Monday, extending last week’s gains as the US and Iran exchanged strikes in the Middle East, fueling concerns over prolonged disruptions to energy flows from the region. The US targeted three Iranian oil tankers over the weekend in retaliation for ballistic missile attacks on US Navy warships. In response, Tehran said a new restricted zone would be established outside the Strait of Hormuz, including a US Navy blockade line and extending into parts of the Persian Gulf. Meanwhile, US Energy Secretary Chris Wright said the US would maintain its naval presence in the Middle East, including the blockade designed to curb Iranian oil exports and help ensure the safe passage of commercial vessels through Hormuz. Fighting between the two sides resumed last week after roughly a month of relative calm, pushing oil prices about 10% higher as markets reassessed geopolitical and supply risks.

【伊朗稱將在霍爾木茲海峽之外設立禁區】據伊朗伊斯蘭共和國廣播電視臺6日報道，伊朗最高國家安全委員會秘書穆赫辛·雷扎伊說，伊朗將宣佈在霍爾木茲海峽之外設立一個禁區，進入船隻將受到制裁。 雷扎伊接受採訪時說，伊朗將在未來數日公佈這一新設立的禁區，範圍將從美國海軍“封鎖線”延伸至波斯灣部分海域，任何進入這一範圍的船隻都將被伊方列入制裁名單。 雷扎伊還表示，美國總統特朗普聲稱霍爾木茲海峽仍處於開放狀態是“謊言”。美方正試圖投入大量資金，以“走私方式”讓船隻透過有關海域，這些船隻通常會遭到伊方打擊。伊朗目前沒有決定擊沉這些船隻，因為它們裝載石油，被擊沉可能對地區環境造成損害。 談及國內物資供應，雷扎伊說，所謂美國經濟封鎖將導致伊朗發生大規模饑荒的說法是“巨大謊言”，伊朗政府已儲備足夠的糧食和基本物資。他同時表示，伊朗仍在出口石油，相關收入也能夠匯回國內。（新華社）

【香港第11批銀色債券認購創雙新高 47.8萬人認購涉資近1200億港元】由香港特區政府發行的第11批銀色債券於9月4日截止認購，認購人數及認購金額雙雙創下歷史新高。香港特區政府發言人公佈的初步資料顯示，本次銀債認購人數約47.8萬人，認購金額約1197.7億港元，較2025年發行的上一批銀債分別增加約28%及約22%。公開資訊顯示，這批銀債保底年化收益率為4.25%，年期為3年，每半年派息一次，每手1萬港元，目標發行額為500億港元，可視認購反應加碼至上限550億港元。由於保底息率明顯高於市面上大部分港元定期存款利率，加上銀債具有政府擔保、本金保障、穩定派息等特點，對追求穩健收益的年長投資者極具吸引力。香港特區政府發言人表示，鑑於銀債受年長市民歡迎，預計最終發行額將由500億港元提高至上限550億港元。香港特區政府計劃於9月11日公佈配發結果，9月15日正式發行。

【2026年基金半年報首次披露新指標，42只“長跑基”讓九成基民賺到錢】2026年基金半年報首次披露了“近一年盈利投資者數量佔比”這一衡量基民持有體驗的新指標。資料顯示，全市場主動權益基金中，該佔比超90%的基金僅佔47.73%。 證券時報基金研究院將範圍縮小至公募行業金字塔尖的“雙十”基金經理，即擔任基金經理年限超10年（截至9月4日，下同）、管理基金綜合年化收益率超10%（共管產品以排在第一位的基金經理為準）的群體。在這些基金經理管理的52只近10年年化收益率超10%的“長跑基”中，有42只基金近一年盈利投資者數量佔比超過90%，佔比高達80.8%，遠超全市場整體水平。 這些持有體驗較佳的基金涉及30多位基金經理，各有各的長跑之道：有人靠“長情”，以低換手和穩定持倉讓基民看得懂、拿得住；有人靠剋制，以極致分散和嚴控回撤讓基民拿得穩、不心慌；有人靠敏銳，以產業週期的精準把握捕捉景氣向上的機會。（人民財訊）

【高盛：戰術性做多日元的理由已有所增強】高盛表示，由於市場預期日本央行將收緊貨幣政策、日本資本流動可能發生變化以及進一步干預的風險，在日元近期走強之後，戰術性做多日元的理由已有所增強。“從更長週期來看，不對稱性明顯改善，對偏好風險的投資組合而言，做空美元/日元已成為久違的、更具吸引力的對沖策略，”包括Kamakshya Trivedi在內的策略師在報告中寫道。高盛指出，日本央行行長植田和男“基本上證實了市場”對9月加息的“預期”，而關於日本政府養老金投資基金(GPIF)可能將資產配置轉向國內資產的猜測，則增加了資本外流減少的前景。儘管全球背景仍對日元構成阻力，且“在沒有出現增長擔憂的情況下應該會持續下去”，但支援性的國內政策轉變“在曾是主要下行壓力源之後，如今應產生更大的影響”。日本決策者“似乎正致力於運用現有手段吸引資本流入”，同時保留了進行進一步干預的充足空間。

### 2026-09-07 07:23:57  #公司

【近期南非連續發生涉南僑胞被綁架案件，中使館提醒提高安全防護意識】據“中國駐南非大使館”公眾號訊息，近期，南非約翰內斯堡的塞爾比、傑米斯頓地區連續發生涉我僑胞被綁架案件。綁匪以經營店鋪、酒鋪的僑胞為主要目標，以索要贖金為作案目的，選擇受害人送貨途中或門店附近作案。我僑胞人身財產安全受到嚴重威脅。駐南非使館提醒廣大僑胞，務必密切關注當地安全和治安形勢，進一步提高安全防護意識，主動防範被綁架風險：一、加強安防措施。儘量選擇安保設施齊全、周邊環境安全的社群經營居住。已在較高風險地區從業僑胞儘快搬離，加大店鋪周圍和出行途中安防措施，運用遠端監控等手段看店，配置“一鍵報警”等行動式防範裝置手段，加強與親友和鄰居日常聯絡。提前規劃安全避險和脫離路線，降低被綁架風險。二、確保出行安全。合理調整店鋪營業時間，避免清晨夜間單獨出行。時常變換通勤及進出貨路線和時間，進出經營居住場所和行車出行時多留意周邊環境和陌生人，防止遭人尾隨。如發現尾隨情形，迅速駕車前往附近警局或加油站等相對安全場所，並視情向華人社團求助。避免前往綁架案件安全風險高、社會治安不靖地段。三、依法合規經營。遵守當地法律，儘量僱傭當地人，確保合法用工，加強對員工及臨時僱員的情況瞭解和日常觀察，妥善處理勞資關係。不隨意透露自己的住址、行程、車輛、作息和財務資訊，避免因非法用工經營引發案件。不隨身攜帶大量現金，不在公共場合談論公司經營、貨款往來和家庭財產情況。四、沉著冷靜應對。如遇到危險，儘量保持冷靜，以人身安全為重，找準時機報警或逃脫。如無法逃脫，不與綁匪衝突、不透露家底、不允諾贖金，相機靈活應對，以便警方營救。受害人家屬應及時報警，並聽從警方談判專家意見，不輕易許諾高額贖金，主動向警方提供相關細節，積極配合警方開展偵查，爭取儘快解救被綁架人員。駐南非使館將繼續推動南非警方儘快偵辦涉中國公民案件，切實維護中國公民安全和合法權益。

【吉利控股集團董事長李書福：“汽車產業是沒有盡頭的馬拉松”】近日，吉利控股集團董事長李書福辭任吉利汽車控股有限公司董事會主席及執行董事職務，將這家上市公司接力棒交給職業經理人團隊。作為我國最早一批民營汽車企業，吉利汽車在核心技術自主研發、海外併購佈局等領域走在前列。

李書福表示：這是一個高度競爭的產業，汽車產業是沒有盡頭的馬拉松，每天都在你追我趕，拼搏向前。我們一直在打基礎、練內功，透過自主創新、海內外併購，尤其是在技工培訓等各種人才培養方面，下了很大決心，投入了大量的資金，逐漸積累起人才優勢、技術優勢，形成了獨具特色的企業制度和文化。這些都是資源和實力，讓我們有能力把想法變成現實。

李書福還表示：汽車產業是一個非常嚴肅的產業，營造公平透明的競爭氛圍對產業的健康發展至關重要。做得好不如說得好，這確實傷害了幹實事人的感情。吉利堅持一切經營活動都不得觸碰法律的紅線和道德的底線，嚴格規定不能有水軍，不能表面一套背後一套，這是企業的底線。（人民日報）

【特朗普社媒驚現“4小時27帖”大刷屏：魔改伊朗地圖、宣稱擁有月球】美東時間週日中午至下午4時許，現任美國總統唐納德·特朗普在其創辦的社交媒體平臺 Truth Social 上再次開啟了罕見的“高頻刷屏”模式，在短短約4個小時內高調發布了多達27條內容，其中包含大量由人工智慧（AI）生成的諷刺與政治暗示圖片。根據海外媒體披露的動態詳情，特朗普此輪發布的27條動態主題五花八門，涵蓋了國際政治、改名領土、自我神化以及對國內政敵的猛烈炮轟。

國際摩擦與地緣宣示： 特朗普在針對中東局勢的動態中，釋出了一張將伊朗國家地圖魔改成自己側臉的AI圖片，並一度將該圖設為其個人頭像。此外，他還發布了多張轟炸伊朗原油樞紐哈爾克島、美軍繳獲油輪的AI虛擬畫面。更令人瞠目結舌的是，他還上傳了一張月球照片，並簡單粗暴地配文宣稱“月球是我們的”。

改名熱潮與政策宣導： 在涉及本土及周邊地理內容中，特朗普放出圖片建議將新墨西哥州更名為“新美國”，並利用AI影象表達了對重新命名安大略湖的意圖。在展示國家武裝力量方面，他高頻釋出多張由AI渲染的“美國太空軍制服樣式圖”及自己身穿黑色極簡科幻制服指揮外太空海戰的圖集。

自吹自擂與政敵嘲諷： 特朗普在此輪動態中堅稱自己“將被作為歷史上最偉大的總統而銘記”。而在抨擊民主黨方面，他則直接使用了一張AI生成的、標有“民主黨”字樣的泰坦尼克號沉沒影象來表達對政敵的看衰。

值得一提的是，就在此番瘋狂刷屏發生前，特朗普的社媒賬號剛剛上線了“獨家內容優先訪問權（Premium Access）”功能，訂購定價高達每月10萬美元。外界普遍猜測，此輪極其荒誕卻極具流量效應的“AI圖片大爆發”，或許與其進一步將其社媒影響力“變現”的商業目的密切相關。目前，白宮和相關發言人尚未對總統這一極具個人風格、虛實結合的“社媒風暴”發表正式評論。

【中美戰區司令會晤釋放的訊號，臺灣要聽懂】根據2026年5月美國總統特朗普訪華時的雙方共識，習近平主席將於今年秋季對美國進行國事訪問。進入9月，中美互動備受關注。對臺灣更是如此。 據新加坡《聯合早報》報道，8月31日至9 月2日，第28屆印太國防軍司令會議在加拿大維多利亞舉行。會議期間，中國人民解放軍東部戰區司令員楊志斌與美軍太平洋司令部司令帕帕羅舉行首次雙邊會談。雙方討論保持兩軍高層溝通暢通的重要性，以降低誤判風險，並推動雙方部隊以安全、專業方式行動。 這場會面之所以格外引人注目，關鍵在於雙方的身份。東部戰區直接擔負臺海方向的戰略任務；太平洋司令部則是美國在西太平洋進行軍事部署、推進盟友協作和介入地區事務的重要中樞。 兩位司令的會面，代表中美兩軍在最敏感方向恢復戰區層級面對面溝通。這意味著雙方正在最容易發生摩擦的領域，建立必要的聯絡與管控，為防止局勢失控設定底線。臺灣真正該聽懂什麼 中美戰區司令會晤，對臺灣釋放了三層清晰訊號。 第一，臺海不能成為中美戰略誤判的引爆點。中美都不願因為軍事摩擦而直接衝突，這為地區穩定增加了一層緩衝。 第二，臺海更不能成為 “臺獨” 挑釁的試驗場。中美兩軍溝通是為了管控風險，絕不是為了容忍風險製造者不斷突破紅線。 第三，臺海和平的根基不在外部承諾，而在兩岸關係本身。外部溝通可以減少危險，卻不能解決臺灣問題；真正能為臺灣同胞帶來長久安寧的，是堅持一箇中國原則、反對“臺獨”分裂、推動兩岸恢復交流與對話，祖國統一才是臺海和平之根。 （環球網）

NZX 50 Trades Slightly Lower to Start the Week — New Zealand stocks dropped 21 points, or 0.2%, to 13,954 in Monday morning trade, after gaining in the previous session, tracking a decline on Wall Street on Friday amid expectations that the Fed would raise interest rates at its upcoming meeting following stronger-than-expected labor data. Traders were cautious ahead of China’s CPI and PPI data, as well as trade figures for August, due later this week. Investors also monitored developments in the Middle East conflict after Tehran said it had targeted oil tankers in response to US strikes. Traders also continued to assess Friday's statement from RBNZ Assistant Governor Karen Silk, which signaled that New Zealand’s central bank is more likely to wait until December before raising interest rates again. The tech, consumer discretionary, energy, and financial sectors mainly weighed on the index, with early losers including Ryman Healthcare (-1.0%), ANZ Group (-0.8%), Infratil (-0.8%), and Channel Infrastructure (-0.6%).

【Iran to raise gasoline price for heavy users from Tuesday】Iran will raise the gasoline price for consumers using more than 110 liters per month, effective Tuesday, government spokesman Mohajerani said. First- and second-tier quotas, including the 110‑liter band, will remain unchanged; the third-tier price will increase to 100,000 Iranian rials per liter (about $0.04 at the free-market exchange rate). Mohajerani said the adjustment, driven by the “current situation” including the war and US sanctions, will only affect monthly users above 110 liters. Other motorists can still buy up to 60 liters at 15,000 rials/liter and an additional 50 liters at 30,000 rials/liter. Iran’s gasoline remains among the cheapest globally; authorities previously delayed hikes over fears of sparking protests after 2019 unrest.

【俄外交部 ：高市要求俄拆除紀念碑的言論“荒謬且無視歷史”】俄羅斯外交部發言人扎哈羅娃6日回應日本首相高市早苗要求俄拆除戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑言論時說，這如同要求“不要颳風和太陽停止發光”一樣荒謬，不要把無視本國曆史的“病”傳染給其他國家。俄遠東哈巴羅夫斯克市4日為一座紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑舉行揭幕儀式。該紀念碑是俄目前最大的紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑，由俄羅斯軍事歷史學會與哈巴羅夫斯克邊疆區政府合作建成。紀念碑中央是一尊15米高的蘇聯士兵雕像，代表擊敗日本關東軍主力的蘇聯紅軍。士兵手持長槍，旁邊一座繪有菊花圖案的界碑被砸成兩半。扎哈羅娃6日表示：“我們的領土由先輩守護下來，我們的歷史基於事實，我們的記憶鐫刻在紀念碑上。”扎哈羅娃特別指出，日本方面無視本國曆史，別把這種“病”傳染給其他國家。俄國家杜馬（議會下院）議員奧列格·馬特維切夫稱，二戰是以日本無條件投降結束的，哈巴羅夫斯克的紀念碑正是為此設立，在這一大背景下，日方要求拆除這座紀念碑“很可笑”。俄羅斯軍事歷史學會副主席奧夫西延科表示，該紀念碑上被搗毀的菊花圖案，正是軍國主義日本的象徵，如此設計紀念碑是正確和符合史實的。 (央視新聞)

### 2026-09-06 21:10:12  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Macro & Rates #US Economy #WTI #Impact bearish #Brent

【U.S. economic pressure appears to be working; Iran's leverage in Strait of Hormuz wanes】After six months of conflict-driven market volatility, U.S. maritime blockades and tighter sanctions have materially curtailed Iranian oil exports and access to foreign exchange, leaving Tehran under one of the gravest economic squeezes in its history, according to Iranian and regional sources. U.S. and regional officials judge the pain could compel Iran to allow free navigation through the Strait of Hormuz, arguing Iran’s economic losses now exceed the disruption it can inflict. Measures to choke exports have reduced government revenue, and Tehran’s attempts to disrupt shipping have not triggered a sustained global shock: oil markets have re-priced and alternative supplies continue to flow. Iranian analyst Arash Aziz says the balance has tilted slightly against Iran—Tehran cannot fully close the strait and U.S. maritime pressure is having an effect. It is unclear whether the pressure will force concessions; Tehran has not abandoned its demands, but mediators are reportedly discussing a new proposal to break the impasse.

### 2026-09-06 21:06:45  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500 #Impact mixed

【CITIC Securities: Market still range-bound; don’t panic over overseas rates】CITIC Securities says whether the Fed hikes in September is not decisive for long-end rates or equity direction. In an era of rapid AI development, demand for government bonds as a traditional safe asset is likely structurally declining; the sell-off in US and European sovereigns reflects economic and market dynamics, not a reliable signal for near-term equity moves. Overall, markets remain range-bound; investors should not panic over overseas rates or overreact to stronger-than-expected market responses to rate moves. The deeper driver of widening long-end domestic-foreign spreads is a capital supply–demand mismatch; with cross-border goods trade facing greater friction, resolving the impasse may depend on outbound financial flows.

### 2026-09-06 19:05:46  #其他

【預警視窗僅幾分鐘 吉隆泥石流災害鏈源頭在境外是監測最大短板】針對吉隆泥石流災害中堰塞湖、冰岩崩—泥石流鏈式災害等風險監測難題，中國科學院成都山地研究所研究員蘇鵬程在見面會上進行了解答。他表示，此次冰岩崩—泥石流災害鏈源頭位於尼泊爾境內藍塘裡壤峰北坡，在海拔約5200米處發生的冰岩崩高位快速墜落，沖刷山體形成巨型泥石流，沿普熱普藏布和東林藏布高速衝擊約22公里後直達海拔1800米左右的我國吉隆口岸，整個過程僅6～7分鐘時間，對下游造成極其嚴重的危害。蘇鵬程研究員結合吉隆口岸實際情況，建議未來可以加強以下幾個方面的工作：第一、強化喜馬拉雅山地區跨境冰岩崩—泥石流這類冰凍圈複合災害鏈成災機理研究，深化災害鏈的早期預警訊號研判和識別。第二，構建跨喜馬拉雅山地區空-天-地一體化的非接觸式監測體系，降低對極高海拔源區實地布站的依賴。第三，推進跨境科研合作與預警協同機制建設。冰凍圈複合災害鏈的發生是自然過程不受國界約束，上游危險源位於境外是本次監測最大短板。（央視新聞）

【衛星遙感監測顯示：東林藏布流域冰湖面積目前未見異常】9月6日，西藏自治區吉隆縣“8·26”泥石流災害應急救援指揮部舉行的中外媒體見面會上介紹，災害發生後，我國組織相關領域專家團隊和技術力量，對東林藏布流域面積大於0.1平方公里的11處冰湖持續展開衛星遙感監測，目前冰湖面積未見異常。近年來，西藏自治區基於多源遙感影像、外業調查及參考冰川相關研究資料資料，組織開展了西藏自治區冰川冰湖資源本底調查。調查顯示，共有冰湖3.3萬多個。西藏按照“重點突出、精準防控”原則，構建技防+人防一體化監測體系，優先選取187個高風險冰湖，開展衛星遙感監測，對其中49個高危冰湖進行地面監測和無人機偵測。建立氣象、水文、自然資源、應急等多部門資料共享機制。定期組織專家對冰湖風險進行會商研判。建立短臨預警“叫應”機制，制定冰湖緊急避險預案。

【中信證券港股投資建議：紅利配置需兼顧籌碼韌性與盈利驗證】中信證券研報指出，全球長端利率繼續上行，港股高股息相對海外無風險資產的收益率優勢有所收窄，配置上應進一步考察現金流穩定性、盈利確定性和分紅可持續性，並關注南向持倉佔比較高、外資邊際定價權相對較低的細分方向。建議優先關注銀行、煤炭、物業管理和燃氣，其中銀行的中報盈利、近期預期修正和籌碼結構相對穩健，煤炭兼具盈利增長與預期上修，物業管理和燃氣則具備較好的股息保護。石油石化、海運和交通運輸可作為階段性配置方向，但需關注地緣風險及週期波動；電信和公用事業的高股息與籌碼結構有助於緩解外圍利率衝擊，但當前盈利預期偏弱，更適合作為防禦性配置。內需相關紅利行業仍需等待基本面企穩。

【明確養老機構重大事故隱患判定程式 兩項養老機構強制性國家標準釋出】市場監管總局近日釋出《養老機構重大事故隱患判定準則》強制性國家標準，該標準在總結全國養老機構安全管理經驗的基礎上，明確了養老機構重大事故隱患的判定程式，這套規則既科學又實用，能幫助一線管理人員迅速“對號入座”，精準識別風險。《養老機構重大事故隱患判定準則》，主要內容包括：範圍、規範性引用檔案、術語和定義、判定程式、判定規則、直接判定要素、綜合判定要素等7個部分。近日釋出的《養老機構基本規範》強制性國家標準將於2027年8月1日起實施。該標準對養老機構從設立、運營到退出的全週期應具備的安全與服務質量劃出底線。標準針對養老機構的選址與建築、設施與裝置、入院出院管理、基礎服務、安全管理、運營管理、評價與改進等7個方面，系統提出了90項具體要求。

【Kaiyuan Securities: Next-stage returns to come from intra-tech stock selection】Kaiyuan Securities: Short-term crowding has partially unwound and STAR 50 implied volatility has fallen, creating intra-tech segmentation opportunities. Strategy: maintain balanced tech exposure and use rebalancing. Rebalancing focus: small/micro caps (CSI 2000, Wind micro-cap stocks); sectors with upside net-profit surprises — electronics, defense & military, computers, oil & petrochemicals, nonferrous metals, non-bank financials; rotation targets — nonferrous, basic chemicals, new energy, agriculture, pharmaceuticals and selected midstream manufacturing such as shipbuilding; and high-dividend defensives (banks, utilities, power) for favorable risk/reward in volatile markets. Medium term: technology remains the main theme, broad beta gains will be harder and returns will increasingly come from within-tech stock selection. Positioning should target names combining a second-leg re-ignition and narrative traction: AI materials, domestic compute-power chain and upstream overseas compute-chain suppliers (PCB, optical-module upstream), programming/enterprise AI agents and applications, plus tech spillover beneficiaries including power equipment, power utilities, energy metals and liquid-cooling solutions.

【方正證券：9月中旬之後向上的合力逐漸具備，市場有望迎來新一輪修復行情】方正證券指出，當前市場風格不存在一邊倒的基礎環境，即持續偏向成長或價值的可能性都不大，分子端業績景氣指向成長佔優，分母端海外利率指向價值佔優，當前價值風格已經連續三週跑贏成長，風格切換的基礎已經具備。海外高利率近期對風格影響較大，隨著美聯儲9月中旬議息會議的臨近，不論是加息還是按兵不動，高利率對科技成長衝擊最大的時候正在過去。從市場整體而言，中報表明全A非金融盈利能力出現向上拐點，市場向下調整的空間有限，打破磨底格局的驅動力可能來自於元首會面對風險偏好的提振，以及後續內需政策的釋放，9月中旬之後向上的合力逐漸具備，屆時市場有望迎來新一輪修復行情。逢低佈局，積極關注三方面配置機會，一是AI的產業趨勢和景氣度仍較為堅實，擁擠度回落之後配置價值顯著提高，科技股內部向能漲價、能擴量、有新技術突破的細分方向傾斜，包括海外算力中擁擠度不高、籌碼結構較好的核心標的，以及算力中景氣確定性較強的半導體裝置和材料，此外還可以關注相對低位的AI應用以及恒生科技；二是關注弱美元下HALO資產的佈局機會，美聯儲9月議息會議前後如有擾動將提供更好的佈局機會，聚焦核心資源相關的有色金屬+化工；三是對於貨幣政策變化反應最為直接，業績估值匹配度較好，能夠穩指數的非銀金融。

【中色股份：控股子公司達瑞礦業收到印尼法院通知】中色股份(000758)9月6日公告，公司控股子公司中色印尼達瑞礦業有限公司（簡稱“達瑞礦業”）於9月4日收到印度尼西亞共和國雅加達行政法院電子法庭系統送達的訴訟案件通知，達瑞礦業成為案件第二介入被告。訴訟標的為：印度尼西亞共和國環境部部長/環境控制署署長籤批的2026年第1437號決定書，即《關於達瑞礦業在北蘇門答臘省達瑞縣SilimaPungga-Pungga鄉開展鉛鋅礦採礦活動的環境可行性決定（SKKL）》（簡稱“環境可行性決定批覆”）。訴訟請求為：要求被告暫緩執行2026年1437號環境可行性決定批覆；要求裁定環境可行性決定批覆無效或不產生法律效力；要求被告撤銷環境可行性決定批覆；要求被告承擔相關訴訟費用。截至目前，達瑞鉛鋅礦專案環境可行性決定批覆合法有效，該事項未對公司經營業績產生影響，訴訟最終結果和對公司未來經營業績的影響尚存在不確定性。公司認為達瑞鉛鋅礦專案對環境的影響已經進行了充分的分析論證並經專家評審透過，達瑞礦業將配合印度尼西亞共和國環境部積極應訴，堅決維護公司權益。

【水利部部長李國英赴西藏調研冰湖監測預警及風險防範工作】李國英指出，青藏高原冰湖分佈廣、數量多，且多位於江河源頭。受全球氣候變化影響，潰洩風險不斷累積，風險暴發往往引發鏈式災害，嚴重威脅下游安全。要全面摸清青藏高原冰湖情況，充分利用高分衛星遙感、無人機載雷達等現代化監測手段，科學佈設水文、地震動儀等監測設施，以及壩體位移形變、滲流滲壓、應力應變等智慧感測裝置，構建“天空地水工”一體化的綜合監測感知系統，精準掌握冰湖發展變化，及時預警潰洩風險。李國英強調，要堅持系統觀念，以流域為單元，深入分析冰湖潰洩的致險、承險、防險要素，充分考慮各環節風險鏈式疊加放大效應，“一湖一策”“一河一策”制定防範應對方案。要建立健全上下游風險隱患監測預警和響應聯動機制，落細落實鏈式災害風險防範各環節各方面責任和措施。

【AI short-drama production prices plunge, industry consolidation underway】AI short-form drama production has surged this year as technical barriers fell and capacity expanded, driving per-minute production quotes down from ~5,000 yuan to several hundred yuan—approaching cost—while bespoke projects still command 10,000–20,000 yuan/min. Key market rules: individuals who obtain household registration in Hainan may exercise Hainan resident rights to purchase commercial housing from the date of registration; active‑duty military serving in Hainan (officers, NCOs, conscripts) receive the same purchase rights; employees of companies registered in Hainan who actually work and live on the island may buy one commercial housing unit in Haikou; multi‑child families (two or more children) may purchase one additional unit beyond current permitted quotas. ### 2026-09-06 12:24:47  #資料 #市場 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【9月加息？美銀、花旗分歧明顯】全球投資者將緊盯美國8月通脹資料，因為這些資料可能決定美聯儲是否會在9月晚些時候加息。然而，華爾街主要機構對這份報告的解讀存在明顯分歧：美銀證券認為資料將足夠強勁，支援9月加息落地；花旗則預計核心通脹將進一步降溫，美聯儲更可能按兵不動。據追風交易臺，美銀證券預測美國8月核心CPI環比上漲0.22%，對應核心PCE約0.24%，年率將升至3.4%，認為這一水平足以說服美聯儲主席沃什通脹尚未得到充分控制，從而支援再度加息。然而，花旗預測美國8月核心CPI環比僅增0.184%，年率降至2.3%——若成真，將是2021年4月核心CPI突破2%以來的最低年率讀數，足以令大多數官員選擇維持利率不變。花旗指出，此次8月PCE（個人消費支出物價指數）資料的不確定性高於尋常——美聯儲對部分關鍵分項進行了方法論調整，這意味著CPI與PPI資料向PCE的常規對映關係在本月變得不夠清晰，同時過去五年的PCE歷史資料也將面臨修訂，預計將使核心PCE年率下調逾30個基點至3.0%左右。這一技術性因素增加了市場對最終PCE讀數的解讀難度。（券商中國）

【印尼一景區山火蔓延 我領館提醒中國公民暫停前往】9月6日，中國駐泗水總領館發文提醒中國公民關注伊真火山森林火情。宣告稱，近期，印尼東爪哇省伊真火山保護區發生森林及山地火情，火勢已蔓延至火山口核心徒步遊覽線路，火情持續發展、現場風險較高。根據東爪哇省自然資源保護局（BBKSDA）官方通知，自2026年9月4日起，伊真火山景區、登山步道及全部旅遊活動已全面臨時關閉，恢復開放時間另行通知。為切實保障領區中國公民人身安全，中國駐泗水總領館特此緊急提醒：一、立即暫停前往所有計劃前往外南夢、邦多沃索地區伊真火山景區的中國遊客、登山愛好者及出行人員取消行程、切勿前往。火情期間區域存在山體火情蔓延、濃煙窒息、道路封閉、救援受阻等多重安全隱患。二、及時調整行程已預訂伊真火山遊覽、登山、地接、交通住宿行程的公民，儘快聯絡旅行社、平臺、酒店及時退票、改期或改簽，避免滯留及不必要損失。三、嚴守當地規定當前景區全線封閉，嚴禁私自繞行、違規進山、夜間徒步等行為。密切關注當地消防、災害管理部門及景區官方釋出的解封資訊，待官方正式解禁後再安排出行。（央視新聞）

【教育部明確校服購買遵循自願 嚴禁未經集體決策選購校服】《人民日報》9月6日報道，近段時間，各地中小學校服採購工作陸續啟動。校服質量關係學生健康，家長們十分關注。 今年，教育部會同相關部門持續推進中小學校園餐、教輔、校服“三項整治”。3月，新版基礎教育規範管理負面清單釋出，嚴禁強制購買校服，利用購買校服牟利、侵害群眾利益。 該負責人介紹，學校應當深入論證，並與家長委員會充分溝通，以確定是否選用校服。嚴禁未經集體決策選購校服，不得以校園活動、儀容儀表等為由設定穿著門檻。一般來說，校服採購每3年一個時段，即小學一至三年級、四至六年級、初中階段、高中階段，分時段在起始年級採購。畢業年級不組織採購校服。（人民日報）

【無界動力張玉峰：具身智慧5年後有望對標成年人勞動能力】2026年亞布力論壇夏季年會於9月4-6日在成都召開。會議期間，新浪科技與無界動力創始人兼CEO張玉峰展開對話。他表示，如果將具身智慧與智慧駕駛行業做類比，當前的具身智慧大約處於2019年前後的自動駕駛階段。他預判5年後，具身智慧有機會達到一個成年人“面向勞動、能思考會勞動”的水平，並且可依賴、可信賴。在家庭場景方面，張玉峰認為，機器人達到成年人的能力大約需要5年時間，但這並不意味著5年後才能進家庭。“掃地機器人早早就進了家庭，把單一事情做得很好，它只需要泛化背景。最近大家想讓掃地機長出一個手臂，能泛化物體、撿襪子、撿積木。”他判斷，對於人形和半人形機器人，未來兩三年左右時間可以嘗試進入家庭，但需要3到5個殺手級技能。“比如把髒衣服拿到洗衣機裡、把乾淨的衣服拿出來晾上，這可能已經是一個殺手級應用。再比如把殘食倒掉、把盤子放進洗碗機......3到5個高頻剛需的事情，大機率兩三年內是可以做到的。”張玉峰說。（新浪科技）

【河南出臺15項金融舉措 精準支援城市更新行動】近日，國家金融監督管理總局河南監管局、中共河南省委金融委員會辦公室、河南省住房和城鄉建設廳聯合釋出《關於印發進一步加強金融支援城市更新有關措施的通知》（以下簡稱《通知》），推出15項支援措施，進一步健全多元化投融資方式，引導各類金融機構強化創新金融產品和服務，為城市更新行動提供全週期、全鏈條、全方位的高質量金融服務。聚焦群眾關切與城市所需，讓金融支援更加精準。針對城市更新涉及面廣、專案型別多、融資需求差異大等特點，《通知》聚焦五大領域分類施策，把更多的金融資源投向群眾最關心、城市最需要的領域。在服務完整社群方面，用好房地產“白名單”機制，加大對“好房子”的金融支援力度，支援收購存量商品房用作保障性住房，鼓勵創新個人信貸產品支援居民原拆原建、自主更新，降低居民籌資壓力。在市政基礎設施方面，圍繞老舊管網更新改造、“平急兩用”公共設施建設等專案加大中長期融資供給。在歷史文化資源保護利用方面，立足河南厚重歷史文化資源，統籌推進修復修繕和活化利用，強化金融要素保障，將歷史文化資源轉化為地方發展動能。在城市精細化治理方面，聚焦“微改造、微治理”場景開發面向社會運營主體的普惠金融服務。在新型城市基礎設施建設方面，引導金融機構將新型城市基礎設施建設專案、城市基礎設施生命線專案納入優先支援範圍，助力提升城市韌性水平。（河南釋出）

Funding principle is owner contributions primary, fiscal subsidies secondary and social capital complementary; eligible projects may apply for relevant policy funds, social capital participation is supported, and policy banks and commercial lenders are encouraged to provide compliant medium‑ to long‑term, low‑interest special loans. Owners in financial hardship with no other housing can be temporarily accommodated through allocation of affordable housing during construction.

【中共湖北省委十二屆十二次全體會議舉行 省委常委會主持會議 關志鷗講話】中國共產黨湖北省第十二屆委員會第十二次全體會議，於2026年9月5日在武漢舉行。出席全會的有省委委員78人，候補省委委員1人。省紀律檢查委員會常務委員會委員、有關方面負責同志和部分省第十二次黨代會代表列席會議。

全會強調，要聚力科技創新，打造原始創新策源地。建強國家戰略科技力量，構建梯次銜接、運轉高效的實驗室體系，全面強化基礎研究，加快推進關鍵核心技術攻關，建設集展示、收儲、撮合、交易、轉化等功能於一體的光谷科技成果轉化中心，努力打造具有全國影響力的科技創新高地。

全會強調，要加快產業創新，打造現代化產業體系。最佳化提升汽車、鋼鐵、化工等傳統產業，培育壯大光電子資訊、生物醫藥及高階醫療器械、新型能源、時空地理資訊等新興產業，前瞻佈局量子科技、具身智慧、6G、腦機介面等未來產業。全面激發產業創新活力，培育更多具有全球競爭力的一流企業。

全會強調，要加強金融創新，打造科創金融品牌。實施“科技金融+”行動，構建全生命週期的多元化金融服務體系。深化“一鏈一金融”服務模式，鼓勵科技金融創新產品與服務在鄂先行先試。深化財政金融協同聯動，擴圍實施財政金融協同促內需一攬子政策。

全會強調，要推動區域創新，打造融合發展共同體。最佳化國土空間佈局，完善利益分配等合作新機制，促進武漢都市圈一體化協同融合發展，打造高質量發展增長極。以武漢都市圈帶動全省區域創新聯動發展，推動宜昌、襄陽、江漢平原等地打造各具特色的高質量發展增長點，推動十堰、恩施、神農架等地強化生態保護，實現生態優先、綠色發展。加強中部地區協同創新，主動融入全國創新鏈。

全會強調，要深化開放創新，打造內陸開放高地。實施花湖機場、天河機場“雙樞紐”提能工程，建設多式聯運國際物流樞紐。建好用好自貿區、綜保區、跨境電商綜試區等開放平臺，拓展對外合作新空間。深入實施“文體旅+”行動，發展文化創意產業，加快建設世界知名的文化旅遊目的地。

全會強調，要強化治理創新，打造一流營商環境。完善創新支援政策，持續加大科技投入力度。構建親清政商關係，做到“有呼必應、無事不擾”。深化“高效辦成一件事”改革，建好用好數智湖北“一張圖”，推動政務服務向“增值賦能”升級。持續推進大財政體系、國資國企、開發區管理體制、生態文明體制等改革。（湖北日報）

【江西遂川湯湖鎮至高坪鎮明坑村道路全線搶通】9月5日上午，江西省吉安市遂川縣防汛抗旱指揮部通報，受颱風"沙德爾"帶來的連日強降雨影響，5日凌晨4時許，遂川縣高坪鎮明坑村石下組突發泥石流災害，造成12棟房屋受損，其中8棟完全損毀、4棟一般受損。目前，已救出3人（其中2人身體狀況良好、1人死亡），尚有11人失聯。經過連續奮戰，湯湖鎮至高坪鎮明坑村道路於6日凌晨1時30分全線搶通，目前正對全路段較狹窄處進行拓寬作業。從左安鎮、湯湖鎮往高坪鎮明坑村方向道路打通後，小車已可通行，搶險裝置正逐步進入災區。凌晨2時，3臺挖掘機已抵達明坑村現場前方指揮部投入作業。此外，隊伍調派動力舟橋、衝鋒舟、機械化橋等裝備趕赴現場，開闢水上救援通道，力爭儘快抵近核心受災點展開搜救。（央視新聞）

【4年銷售額增長近200倍 百年德企“追著金壇投”】9月2日，勞施保施新能源汽車系統整合（江蘇）有限公司在常州市金壇區開業。這是德國勞施保施集團在金壇投資佈局的二期專案，基地全面投產後將具備年產10萬套全主動懸架系統的生產能力，併成為賽力斯汽車（問界系列）的核心供應商。勞施保施總部位於德國巴伐利亞的塞爾布，擁有超過70年的電磁閥研發經驗和35年汽車配件供應經驗。其生產的空氣懸架電磁閥佔據全球市場80%至90%的份額，是細分領域的“隱形冠軍”。2022年勞施保施銷售額約500萬元，2026年預計接近9億元——4年間增長近200倍。勞施保施中國區執行長王紫楠透露，企業今年在手訂單突破10億元，超九成供給國內市場。公司正持續深耕中國新能源市場，加速本地化研發程序，未來將有更多產品在中國實現生產並供應全球。（新華日報）

【中國人民銀行原副行長朱民：中國經濟轉型為亞太提供新機遇】中國人民銀行原副行長、國際貨幣基金組織前副總裁朱民在發言中，闡述中國經濟結構轉型為亞太地區提供的創新開放合作新機遇。朱民指出，中國以科技創新為主要抓手，推出整體經濟調整政策，大力發展科技製造業和創新型消費服務業。上半年中國經濟增速達4.7%，科技產業和新服務業對經濟增長貢獻度達43%。中國推出全面AI戰略，在資料、算力等8個方面提出系統方案。新動能、新科技製造業貢獻上半年經濟增長的50%，鋰電池、積體電路製造等領域增速均超30%。在培育新興產業的同時，中國推進傳統產業數字化轉型。朱民強調，中國經濟結構改革正推動新的全面開放。開放不再侷限於產品和技術，而是轉向高標準制度性開放和營商環境國際對接。同時，在中國深度轉型和新的開放態勢下，亞太地區迎來6大合作機遇：一是推動數字化、智慧化轉型，加強人工智慧、量子科技、生物製造等前沿合作。二是加強產業對接，中國企業出海提升區域供應鏈穩健性。三是中國企業對外投資穩步增長，上半年直接投資達865億美元。四是促進技術普惠共享。五是共同推進綠色轉型。六是推進智慧時代能力建設、人才交流和資訊服務合作。（深圳特區報）

【德國運載火箭第二次發射成功進入軌道】當地時間9月5日晚，德國航天初創企業伊薩爾航空航天公司的“光譜”運載火箭從挪威安島航天港發射升空，併成功進入軌道。這是該型火箭的第二次飛行，也是首次攜帶實際載荷執行任務。此次飛行主要用於驗證火箭在實際飛行條件下的各項關鍵系統。火箭搭載了來自歐洲多所高校和企業的5顆立方星和1項實驗載荷。“光譜”是一款兩級運載火箭，高約28米，主要用於發射小型和中型衛星，最多可將約1噸載荷送入近地軌道。2025年3月，“光譜”火箭進行了首次試飛。火箭在升空約30秒後因飛行異常收到終止指令，隨後在失去動力的情況下墜入海中，未能進入軌道。此次成功入軌意味著歐洲商業航天公司首次從歐洲大陸發射軌道運載火箭併成功進入軌道。歐洲航天局此前表示，發展歐洲商業航天運輸服務有助於增強歐洲自主進入太空的能力。（央視新聞）

【研究：受高溫影響 德國水資源量減少】德國亥姆霍茲地球科學中心日前釋出的研究報告顯示，近幾個月以來，該國水資源量緊張情況進一步加重，高溫是造成這一現象的主要原因。據德新社5日報道，亥姆霍茲地球科學中心3日公佈了最新研究報告。該機構與德國航空航天中心、美國國家航空航天局合作，透過衛星資料監測地表及地下水資源量，以計算其變化。資料顯示，德國水資源量自2015年以來便呈現下降趨勢，今年6月的水資源量較長期平均水平減少約250億噸。（新華社）

【EU sends Frontex patrol vessel to English Channel to support French migrant SAR】The European Border and Coast Guard Agency (Frontex) said it has for the first time deployed a patrol vessel to the English Channel to support French search-and-rescue operations for overloaded migrant boats. The vessel, provided by the Spanish Civil Guard, began operating this week near the French coast and will work with Frontex reconnaissance aircraft that have been deployed over the Channel since 2021. Frontex said the ship’s mandate is limited to supporting French SAR — not intercepting migrants — and will detect, identify and track small boats and relay real-time information to French authorities. Additional vessels from other EU member states are scheduled to join the operation in coming months.

### 2026-09-05 21:15:29  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #US Economy

【Axios: GOP economic confidence plunges】Axios reports GOP voter confidence in the U.S. economy has plunged over the past six months at a pace not seen since the pandemic and at magnitudes comparable to the global financial crisis nearly 20 years ago. Normally the governing party is more optimistic, but Republican views have turned increasingly negative despite a Republican White House, with the election two months away — another warning sign for Trump. The University of Michigan consumer-survey director says party confidence trends usually move in parallel but are diverging now. Republicans had high expectations for government economic measures, including tariffs, but have not seen inflation ease or the price declines Trump promised.

【Northern cold air becomes active; many areas to hit H2 lows】With typhoon impacts on land waning, southern weather will stabilise. Northern cold air has become active; as an upper-level trough shifts east, early next week the cold air will cross the Great Wall, bringing widespread rain and a sharp chill. China’s Central Meteorological Observatory forecasts that from tomorrow through the 9th light-to-moderate rain, locally heavy, will move west-to-east across central–eastern northwest China, North China and north–central Northeast China. Temperatures in the Huanghuai region and areas northward are expected to fall 4–6°C, with parts of Inner Mongolia dropping 8–10°C. Daytime highs and nighttime lows across the north will largely mark their lowest levels since H2; forecast temperature maps show the north shifting from yellow to green.

【Temporary ceasefire around Zaporizhzhia nuclear plant to repair external power lines】IAEA Director General Grossi said on Sept 5 that, after agency-mediated talks, a local temporary ceasefire was implemented around the Zaporizhzhia nuclear plant to enable repairs to damaged transmission lines and restore external power. The plant lost its last external connection on Aug 20 due to military activity and has since relied on emergency diesel generators to provide cooling power for six reactors and spent fuel pools. The IAEA warned diesel reserves are running low and said that if external grid power is not restored or additional fuel cannot be delivered, the plant risks a full-station blackout within days. Under the ceasefire, Ukrainian technicians will de-mine affected areas before beginning repairs; IAEA staff will monitor the work. Grossi said this is the seventh similar temporary ceasefire he has negotiated since the Russia-Ukraine conflict to reduce nuclear safety risks.

【扎波羅熱核電站周邊實施臨時停火 以搶修外部供電線路】國際原子能機構總幹事格羅西9月5日表示，在該機構斡旋下，扎波羅熱核電站周邊再次實施區域性臨時停火，以便搶修受損輸電線路，恢復核電站外部供電。扎波羅熱核電站8月20日因軍事活動失去與最後一條外部輸電線路的連線，此後一直依靠應急柴油發電機為6座反應堆和存放使用過後核燃料的乏燃料池提供冷卻所需電力。國際原子能機構表示，目前核電站柴油儲備已經所剩不多，如果外部供電不能恢復或者無法及時運入更多燃料，未來數日內可能面臨全站斷電風險。根據臨時停火安排，烏克蘭技術人員將在完成相關區域排雷後開始維修輸電線路。國際原子能機構人員將對維修過程進行監測。格羅西表示，這是俄烏衝突以來他為降低扎波羅熱核電站核安全風險斡旋達成的第7次類似臨時停火。

【China's Ministry of Emergency Management sends working group to Jiangxi Suichuan mudslide site】On Sept. 5 a mudflow and landslide struck Shixia group, Mingkeng Village, Gaoping Town, Suichuan County, Jiangxi. The National Disaster Reduction and Relief Commission and the Ministry of Emergency Management initiated level-4 national emergency responses for natural disasters and geological disasters and dispatched a working group to oversee rescue. The ministry ordered all-out search-and-rescue, verification of casualties and missing persons, nearby deployment of communications support and engineering rescue teams, and stepped-up hydrological and geological monitoring and early warning to prevent secondary disasters. 【Chongqing unveils 16 measures to encourage foreign firms' onshore reinvestment】Chongqing's development and reform commission issued 16 measures to encourage foreign-invested enterprises to reinvest domestically, framed on an equal-treatment principle for domestic and foreign capital. The package centers on guiding foreign investment allocation, easing investment and operations, and strengthening safeguards. It explicitly targets participation in Chongqing's 33618 modern manufacturing cluster, prioritizing intelligent-connected NEVs, next-generation electronic information manufacturing, and advanced materials to strengthen and extend industrial chains. The city pledges to fully eliminate foreign-investment access restrictions in manufacturing; qualifying foreign-invested firms' onshore reinvestments in manufacturing projects will receive the same municipal-level project factor guarantees and support policies as domestic investors.

【23家鋰電企業齊聚天府新區 國內首個鋰電全鏈條ESG協作機制啟動】9月3日，第四屆零碳協同創新大會在四川天府新區隆湖畔順利舉行。會上，由天齊鋰業牽頭組建、覆蓋鋰電池全價值鏈的“可持續鋰電池行業工作組”正式啟動，這是中國鋰電池行業首個覆蓋上中下游全鏈條的ESG與可持續發展行業協作機制。工作組將主要聚焦歐盟新電池法規與電池護照技術規範互認、本土碳足跡資料建設、供應鏈可信追溯與安全資料協同、行業可持續敘事與國際合作四大方向，致力於減少重複稽核與重複建設，增強中國鋰電池產業的全球參與度。天齊鋰業將擔任首個輪值主席。目前，工作組已匯聚比亞迪、贛鋒鋰業、格林美、國軒高科、海辰儲能、盛新鋰能、四川動力、天能集團、天齊鋰業、五礦新能、小米集團、欣旺達、雅化集團、紫金礦業等23家鋰電全價值鏈代表性企業，並明確2026年度重點任務，後續將陸續產出併發布行業共識性指引手冊等，系統呈現中國鋰電池行業對全球綠色轉型的貢獻。（每經）

【AI加速嵌入上市險企核心業務】隨著2026年半年報披露完畢，五大A股上市險企的AI（人工智慧）應用呈現出一個鮮明變化：AI正加快從客服、辦公等輔助環節向營銷、核保、理賠、風控等核心業務流程滲透。與此同時，“詞元消耗量”“呼叫次數”等指標開始進入險企半年報之中，部分險企已對外披露AI輔助銷售額、智慧稽核率等經營資料。 這一變化意味著，保險業AI應用正在邁過單純技術部署階段，逐步進入與經營管理深度融合的新階段。受訪專家表示，保險業務具有客戶規模大、流程鏈條長、稽核環節多等特點，這為AI規模化應用提供了豐富場景。隨著應用持續深入，AI的價值也將進一步向促進險企銷售、降低成本、強化風險管控等經營目標延伸。（證券日報）

### 2026-09-05 07:21:36  #市場 #其他

【打新陣營擴容 “兩創板”新股中籤率持續走低】若按照發行日期統計（下同），2026年以來已發行的創業板新股網上平均中籤率為0.0169%，這一數值較此前4年的平均網上中籤率水平均要低，一定程度上意味著今年發行的創業板新股較此前4年更難中籤。2022年至2025年4年間，各年創業板新股網上平均中籤率分別為0.0208%、0.0271%、0.0238%、0.0187%。

除了創業板新股中籤難度加大，近年來科創板新股中籤率整體也呈現走低之勢。資料顯示，自2019年科創板開板以來，其新股網上平均中籤率呈現一定起伏：2019年為0.0505%，2020年和2021年分別降至0.0385%和0.0317%。2022年隨著不少科創板新股破發，科創板新股網上平均中籤率回升至0.0409%，2023年進一步回升至0.0525%。此後兩年，科創板新股中籤率再度連續走低，2024年與2025年分別為0.0428%和0.0364%。

分析人士認為，新股中籤率的變化與新股市場表現、新股中籤收益高低等因素有一定關係，市場自身存在一定自我調節機制。（證券時報）

【美元在強勁非農報告刺激下反彈 惟多空博弈下未改單週頹勢】美元9月開局不利，美聯儲下一步行動受到相互矛盾訊號的夾擊，而即將釋出的關鍵通脹報告也讓交易員坐立難安，與此同時日元的飆升在全球匯市引起震盪。儘管週五公佈的非農資料強於預期，提振了市場對美聯儲本月晚些時候加息的預期，不過彭博美元即期匯率指數本週最終仍收跌0.7%。交易員消化的加息25個基點機率在60%左右，而報告發布約為50%。加拿大帝國商業銀行資本市場策略師Noah Buffam指出，這就提高了下週消費者價格資料的重要性。“美元的下一個走勢可能取決於下週的通脹資料以及它們對9月美聯儲決策的影響，” 他說。
